Result: 在海事轨迹异常检测（AIS运动序列+ERA5环境上下文）的环境敏感绕行场景中，RGFiLM在所有对比方法（上下文无关和上下文条件方法）中取得了最佳的平均F1-FPR权衡。

Conclusion: 显式考虑上下文稀有度是减少上下文敏感异常检测中误报的有效方法。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection aims to identify abnormal behavior conditional on context variables, but practical deployments often face highly imbalanced context distributions where rare regimes can be critical information. Under such frequency bias, context-conditioned models can produce unstable decisions and excessive false alarms in rare contexts. We propose Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation (RGFiLM), a rarity-aware conditioning module that combines feature-wise modulation (i.e., context-conditioned scaling and shifting of hidden features) with a gate controlled by a data-driven rarity score. The rarity score is estimated from the empirical distribution of context variables and regulates how strongly context modulates intermediate representations: the gate becomes more decisive under rare contexts while remaining conservative under frequent contexts. We evaluate RGFiLM on maritime trajectory anomaly detection using AIS motion sequences with ERA5 environmental context in an environment-sensitive detour scenario. When instantiated in a sequential anomaly scoring pipeline, RGFiLM achieves the best mean F1--False Positive Rate (FPR) trade-off among the compared context-agnostic and context-conditioned methods. These results suggest that explicitly accounting for context rarity is an effective approach for reducing false alarms in context-sensitive anomaly detection.

</details>


### [102] [Navigating the Safety-Fidelity Trade-off: Massive-Variate Time Series Forecasting for Power Systems via Probabilistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13338)
*Kaijie Xu,Anqi Wang,Xilin Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PowerPhase是一个基于六个输电网（最高36,964通道）的概率预测基准，远超现有基准规模；PowerForge是一种具有类型特定解码头和变量组间因果桥的场景分位数预测器，在所有网格上取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测基准要么通道数太少（不超过2,000），要么缺乏时间结构或概率评估，无法评估大规模多变量系统上不同通道物理特性和运行约束的影响。

Method: 构建PowerPhase基准，包含六个输电网（2,000–36,964通道），每个轨迹由交流潮流求解产生，并配备约束感知指标（Safety_mBrier, NECV, CVaR-α等）。提出PowerForge，一种场景分位数预测器，具有类型特定解码头和变量组间因果桥。

Result: 在八个基线和三个种子上的实验表明，分布精度和约束满足度对模型排名不同，存在"安全-保真度"权衡。PowerForge在所有网格上取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 大规模多变量概率预测需要在分布精度和约束满足之间进行权衡；PowerPhase为评估这一权衡提供了基准，而PowerForge在其中表现最优。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are increasingly deployed on multivariate systems with distinct channel physics and operational constraints, but existing benchmarks evaluate neither property at scale. Public canonical multivariate benchmarks cap out at 2,000 channels, while power-system benchmarks either lack temporal structure or probabilistic evaluation. We introduce PowerPhase, a probabilistic forecasting benchmark built on six transmission grids ranging from 2,000 to 36,964 jointly forecasted channels, more than an order of magnitude beyond popular canonical multivariate benchmarks. Each target trajectory is the output of an AC power-flow solve, and PowerPhase ships with constraint-aware metrics, including Safety_mBrier, NECV, and CVaR-alpha, that complement CRPS and Distortion. Across eight baselines and three seeds, distributional accuracy and constraint satisfaction rank models differently, a trade-off we term safety-fidelity. We further propose PowerForge, a scenario-based quantile forecaster with type-specific decoding heads and a causal bridge between variable groups, which achieves the best average rank on every grid.

</details>


### [103] [Enhanced Low-Density Region Exploration in Classifier-Guided Diffusion Models Through Modified Reverse Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13347)
*Jagriti Singh,Shekhar Verma,Muneendra Ojha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需额外训练的采样时密度感知引导方法，通过修改分类器梯度将扩散模型采样轨迹导向低置信区域，同时引导向预测真实图像靠近，在不牺牲FID的前提下提升ADM模型在长尾分布上的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类器引导将概率质量集中在高密度类均值附近，导致对类条件分布尾部稀有样本的覆盖不足；现有尾部采样方法需额外训练低密度分类器和判别器，成本高。

Method: 在预训练的条件扩散模型和分类器基础上，于反向采样过程中：(1) 通过修改的分类器梯度将轨迹导向低置信区域，探索低概率样本；(2) 同时引导采样过程向预测真实图像靠近，使生成样本接近真实数据流形。两种引导均在含噪图像上应用梯度，而非预测噪声。

Result: 在ImageNet 64×64上，ADM模型召回率一致提升，FID相当；在256×256上展示了不同引导组合的视觉结果；标准ADM分类器引导结合预测真实图像引导可生成高感知质量样本。

Conclusion: 提出了一种纯采样时的密度感知引导方法，无需额外训练即可改善条件扩散模型在长尾分布上的覆盖能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative models for high-fidelity image synthesis, particularly in their classifier-free guided and classifier-guided forms. However, standard classifier guidance concentrates probability mass around high-density class mean, leading to poor coverage of rare samples in the tails of the class-conditional distributions. Recent work on diffusion-based tail sampling mitigates this by training an additional low-density-seeking classifier with a synthetic-vs-real discriminator, at the cost of additional networks and training. In parallel, a number of samplers and distillation techniques accelerate or refine diffusion sampling, but do not explicitly address long-tail coverage. We propose a purely sampling-time, density-aware extension of classifier-guided conditional diffusion model that targets low-density regions without any additional training. We have applied guidance at noisy images not on predicted noise like most diffusion models. Starting from a pretrained conditional diffusion model and classifier on ImageNet, we modify the guided reverse dynamics by steering trajectories toward low-confidence regions via the modified classifier gradient, and at each time step, we also guide the sampling process toward the predicted real image. 1st guidance helps explore low-probability samples, and 2nd guidance helps to generate samples to be close to the real data manifold. The proposed sampler consistently improves ADM model recall at 64x64 resolution while maintaining a comparable FID, and with a 256x256 ADM model, we showed the results visually with different combinations of both guidance. We also showed that standard ADM classifier guidance, combined with predicted real image guidance, helps generate high perceptual quality samples with a 256x256 ADM model on ImageNet.

</details>


### [104] [VideoMDM: Towards 3D Human Motion Generation From 2D Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13364)
*Amir Mann,Gal Michael Harari,Merav Keidar,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VideoMDM 是一个基于扩散框架的方法，仅从单目视频中提取的精确2D姿态直接训练3D人体运动先验，无需任何3D真值。通过2D重投影损失（等价于期望上的3D监督）和适配2D的3D运动正则化器，在HumanML3D上接近完全3D监督的MDM性能，并在真实视频数据集上生成人类偏好的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体运动生成方法严重依赖3D真值数据，而获取大规模、多样化的3D运动数据成本高昂且困难。作者希望利用大量易获取的单目视频（仅含2D姿态）来训练3D运动先验，从而突破3D数据稀缺的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 使用预训练的2D-to-3D提升器从2D姿态生成近似3D序列作为带噪教师；2) 对这些3D序列加噪后由模型去噪，再将预测结果重投影回2D并与精确2D关键点比较作为监督；3) 理论上证明在温和假设下，深度加权2D重投影损失在期望上等价于直接3D监督；4) 将速度一致性和过参数化表示对齐等标准3D运动正则化器适配到2D设置。

Result: 在HumanML3D上，FID为0.88，接近完全3D监督的MDM（0.54）；在真实视频数据集Fit3D和NBA上，生成的运动在人类偏好评估中表现优异。

Conclusion: VideoMDM证明了仅从2D姿态即可有效学习3D运动先验，显著降低了对3D真值数据的依赖，为利用海量视频数据训练高质量3D运动生成模型开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce VideoMDM, a diffusion-based framework that trains 3D human motion priors directly from accurate 2D poses extracted from monocular videos, without any 3D ground truth. A pretrained 2D-to-3D lifter provides approximate 3D pose sequences that serve as a noisy teacher: these are diffused, denoised by the model in 3D, and supervised in 2D by reprojecting the prediction and comparing against accurate keypoints. We show that, under mild assumptions, a depth-weighted 2D reprojection loss is equivalent in expectation to direct 3D supervision, and we adapt standard 3D motion regularizers - velocity consistency and over-parameterized representation alignment - to this 2D setting. Unlike methods that lift 2D to 3D only at inference, VideoMDM learns a coherent 3D motion manifold during training. On HumanML3D it nearly closes the gap to fully 3D-supervised MDM (FID 0.88 vs 0.54); On real video datasets Fit3D and NBA the method learns to generate motions consistently preferred by humans, with strong quantitative results.

</details>


### [105] [Hölder++: Improving the Quality-Coherence Trade-off in Multimodal VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13381)
*Huyen Vo,María Martínez-García,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态变分自编码器在生成质量与跨模态一致性间的权衡问题，本文提出Hölder++框架，通过精确Hölder池化、共享/私有表示分离及层次化推理，显著改善了该权衡关系，并产生更具结构的潜在空间与更具信息量的共享表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMVAE方法在生成质量与跨模态一致性之间存在权衡：生成真实多样的样本时难以保持语义一致性。近期工作使用近似Hölder池化虽提升了一致性但牺牲了多样性。本文旨在进一步改善这一权衡。

Method: 提出Hölder++，包含三个关键创新：(i) 首次在多模态VAE中实现无近似的精确Hölder池化；(ii) 扩展架构以建模独立的共享表示与私有（模态特有）表示（Hölder+）；(iii) 引入层次化推理进一步增强共享与私有表示的解耦（Hölder++）。

Result: 实验表明Hölder++持续改善了生成质量与一致性的权衡，产生了更具结构的潜在空间，并且学习到的共享表示对下游任务具有信息量。

Conclusion: Hölder++通过精确Hölder池化、共享/私有表示分离及层次化推理，有效解决了多模态VAE中生成质量与一致性的权衡问题，并为下游任务提供了更优的共享表示。

Abstract: Existing approaches for multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) face a trade-off between generative quality and coherence-i.e., they struggle to generate realistic and diverse samples that, at the same time, are semantically consistent across modalities. A recent work shows that using a simple approximation to Hölder pooling as an aggregation method improves coherence over the SOTA MMVAE+, despite assuming a single shared representation across all modalities. Yet, it slightly compromises sample diversity. Inspired by this insight, we propose Hölder++, a novel multimodal VAE that improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off through: (i) the first implementation of Hölder pooling without any approximation for multimodal VAEs; (ii) an extended architecture that models distinct shared and private (i.e., modality-specific) representations (Hölder+); and (iii) hierarchical inference that further enhances the disentanglement between the shared and private representations (Hölder++). Our experiments corroborate that Hölder++ consistently improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off, yields more structured latent spaces, and learns shared representations that are informative for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [106] [Accelerating Speculative Diffusions via Block Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13426)
*Alexander Soen,Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于扩散模型的投机性采样方案，实现了类似LLM中的块验证机制，并提出了无需训练的Free Drafter启发式自投机起草器，最高可获得6.3%的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的投机性解码方法主要针对离散空间（如LLM），而连续扩散模型的投机采样存在残差分布难以高效采样的问题，现有适应方案要么计算效率低，要么依赖替代方案。

Method: 提出一种新的投机采样机制，高效实现了扩散模型的原始投机采样，并适配了LLM中的块验证方法；同时形式化分析了无需训练的Free Drafter启发式自投机起草器。

Result: Free Drafter在无需额外训练且开销极小的条件下，相比现有投机方法获得了最高6.3%的加速比。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于将LLM中的块验证成功适配到扩散模型，并证明了Free Drafter在加速扩散模型推理方面的有效性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up LLM inference by using a draft model to generate tokens, with an acceptance-rejection scheme that ensures that the output matches the target distribution. Adapting this to continuous diffusions is difficult because speculative sampling requires drawing from a residual distribution. While straightforward in discrete spaces, efficiently sampling this residual in continuous space is non-trivial. Consequently, existing diffusion adaptations either use computationally inefficient sampling techniques or rely on an alternative scheme. In this work, we introduce a novel scheme that efficiently implements the original speculative sampling mechanism for diffusion models. Our approach offers a critical advantage over current methods: it enables us to adapt block verification from LLMs to diffusions -- which provably improves the acceptance rate of drafts. Furthermore, we formalize and analyze the Free Drafter, a heuristic self-speculative drafter for diffusions that requires no training. By enabling block verification, our Free Drafter yields up to a 6.3% speedup over existing speculative methods with no additional training and negligible overhead beyond the existing parallel verification pass.

</details>


### [107] [How Much Memory Do We Need? Adaptive Memory Gate for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13443)
*Jihyeon Hur,Yongseok Kwon,Min-Gi Jo,Jeongwhan Choi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMGFNO模型，通过可学习门控机制动态调整记忆权重，在低分辨率下相比固定记忆权重的神经算子方法实现55-79%的nRMSE降低，且学习到的门控值随分辨率提高自动从~0.7降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型神经算子对不同观测条件（如分辨率、物理参数）使用固定记忆权重，而实验表明最优记忆权重随分辨率和粘度变化，固定权重无法同时适应多种设置，限制了模型的适应性和性能。

Method: 提出AMGFNO，通过可学习门控机制动态调节记忆权重，使模型能根据观测条件自适应调整对历史状态的依赖程度。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和Burgers方程上，AMGFNO在低分辨率下nRMSE降低55-79%；学习到的门控值在低分辨率时约为0.7，随分辨率提高自动降至接近零。

Conclusion: 自适应门控机制有效解决了固定记忆权重的局限，使模型能根据观测条件动态调整记忆利用策略，显著提升低分辨率下的预测精度。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for solving time-dependent PDEs. Among recent advances, memory-augmented neural operators explicitly incorporate past states and have achieved remarkable performance under low-resolution observation settings. However, existing approaches apply a fixed memory weight regardless of observation conditions, such as resolution or physical parameters, limiting their adaptability. Our preliminary experiments reveal that optimal memory weight varies with resolution and viscosity, implying that a fixed memory weight cannot simultaneously optimize performance across diverse settings. We propose AMGFNO, which dynamically modulates memory weight through a learnable gate. On the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky and Burgers' equations, AMGFNO achieves 55-79% nRMSE reduction over at low resolution, with the learned gate value automatically decreasing from $\bar{g} \approx 0.7$ to near-zero as resolution increases.

</details>


### [108] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的无监督图聚类框架，通过多轮自学习迭代，综合利用图边信息和节点属性信息进行聚类。每轮中GNN生成节点表示用于聚类，聚类结果影响下一轮的图结构，同时利用原始图构建的上下文图生成节点表示。实验表明该方法在合成数据上优于仅关注网络或属性的算法，多轮学习优于单轮训练，在真实数据上当聚类大小平衡时与最先进方法具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图聚类是基础问题，但传统方法大多只利用网络边信息或节点属性信息之一。随着真实数据中节点属性日益丰富，现有方法未能充分结合两者信息进行聚类。作者希望设计一种能同时有效利用网络结构和节点属性信息的无监督图聚类方法。

Method: 提出一个多轮自学习框架，在每轮中：(1) 使用GNN生成节点表示；(2) 对节点表示进行聚类；(3) 聚类结果用于修改下一轮的图结构；(4) 利用原始图构建的上下文图辅助生成节点表示。整个流程在完全无监督设置下进行，无需标签信息。

Result: 在合成数据上，该方法优于仅利用网络或仅利用属性的算法，尤其在网络信息和属性信息都不充分时优势明显。多轮学习始终优于长时间的单轮训练（即经典GNN图聚类）。在真实数据集上，当聚类大小平衡时，该方法与最先进方法表现相当。

Conclusion: 提出的多轮自学习GNN框架能够有效融合图结构和节点属性信息进行无监督聚类，多轮迭代机制优于传统单轮GNN聚类方法。该方法在平衡聚类场景下具有竞争力，但聚类大小不平衡时可能存在局限。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [109] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于拉普拉斯近似的后验不确定性估计方法，用于预训练分子扩散模型，通过测量噪声预测的变异性来评估生成样本质量，并利用该分数进行测试时过滤以提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D分子生成中已被广泛采用，但缺乏判断生成分子质量高低的原则性信号，因此需要一种能估计每个样本不确定性的方法。

Method: 基于去噪网络的拉普拉斯近似，在生成轨迹上测量噪声预测的变异性，从而得到每个样本的不确定性分数。

Result: 不确定性分数与样本级质量指标呈负相关，能有效反映样本质量；通过过滤低质量样本，可以在测试时提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的后验不确定性估计方法为预训练分子扩散模型提供了一种无需重新训练即可评估样本质量并实现测试时性能提升的有效手段。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [110] [Reinforcement Learning for Neural Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13461)
*Shaivi Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将神经网络编辑形式化为强化学习问题的探索性框架，使用MaskWorld和ShiftWorld两个环境，通过奖励反馈学习编辑策略，在偏见缓解和机器遗忘任务上取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练神经网络编辑需要专门定制的算法，设计这些算法耗时且需要大量精力，缺乏统一的通用框架。

Method: 将神经网络编辑建模为强化学习问题，智能体通过奖励反馈修改模型。设计了两个环境：MaskWorld（权重乘性缩放）和ShiftWorld（权重加性更新）。奖励函数结合了效用保持目标和任务特定编辑目标。

Result: 在机器遗忘任务中，遗忘集准确率降至近0%同时保留集准确率保持在90%以上；在偏见缓解任务中，偏见相关指标提升超过5%且保持通用分类效用。

Conclusion: 神经网络编辑可以转化为强化学习问题，使编辑策略能够通过奖励反馈学习而非为每个任务手动设计算法。

Abstract: Editing pretrained neural networks requires specialized algorithms tailored to specific objectives. Designing such algorithms is often time-consuming and demands significant effort. We present an exploratory framework that formulates neural model editing as a reinforcement learning problem, where agents modify models using reward feedback. We introduce two environments: MaskWorld, where agents scale weights multiplicatively, and ShiftWorld, where agents apply additive weight updates. The reward function combines a utility-preservation objective with a task-specific editing objective, enabling agents to learn targeted modifications while maintaining overall model performance. We evaluate the framework on bias mitigation in text classification and machine unlearning in image classification, both of which traditionally rely on specialized algorithms. Our results show that the learned policies reduce forget set accuracy to nearly 0% while preserving over 90% retain set accuracy on the unlearning task. In the bias mitigation setting, the learned policies improve bias-related performance by more than 5% while maintaining general classification utility. Our findings show that neural model editing can be cast as a reinforcement learning problem, allowing editing policies to be learned from reward feedback rather than manually engineered for each task.

</details>


### [111] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaxProof是一种面向竞赛级数学证明的群体级测试时扩展框架，通过将M3模型作为生成器、验证器、优化器和排序器，对候选证明进行群体搜索和锦标赛选择，在IMO 2025（35/42）和USAMO 2026（36/42）上超越了人类金牌阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将测试时计算扩展（test-time scaling）从标准推理任务推广到数学证明领域，通过群体级搜索提升竞赛级数学证明的生成质量，使其超越人类金牌水平。

Method: 首先训练三种面向证明的核心能力（证明生成、证明验证、基于批判的证明修复），通过深度防御生成式验证器实现低假阳性率，合并为单一M3模型；测试时，将模型作为生成器、验证器、优化器和排序器，对候选证明群体进行搜索，通过锦标赛选择返回最终证明。

Result: 在IMO 2025上达到35/42，在USAMO 2026上达到36/42，均超过人类金牌阈值。

Conclusion: MaxProof证明了群体级测试时扩展能够显著提升数学证明系统的性能，在竞赛级数学问题上首次超越人类金牌水平。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [112] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个超分子化学基准SupraBench，包含四个基础任务（结合亲和力预测、顶级结合剂选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）和一个辅助视觉任务，并发布了16M token的领域语料SupraPMC。基准测试显示LLM在所有任务上仍有显著提升空间，领域适应预训练可提升分布内回归性能但会牺牲严格格式输出，不同任务族呈现差异化困难模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主客体系统设计耗时且缺乏系统性评估LLM在超分子化学推理能力的基准，需要构建标准化评估框架。

Method: 与领域专家合作构建SupraBench基准，设计四项基础推理任务和一项视觉辅助任务；从Europe PMC蒸馏出SupraPMC语料库（16M tokens）；对多种开源和闭源LLM进行基准测试，评估领域适应预训练效果。

Result: LLM在所有任务上表现不足，存在显著提升空间；领域适应预训练（SupraPMC）能改善分布内回归任务但损害严格格式输出；不同任务族的困难模式差异显著，揭示了当前超分子化学推理的具体缺陷。

Conclusion: SupraBench为超分子化学LLM推理提供了首个系统评估基准，揭示了现有模型的差距与失败模式，为未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [113] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF是一种完全无监督的多元时间序列异常检测框架，利用500个随机解析小波特征（覆盖四种小波族）和五个结构化孤立森林，同时检测点异常、分布异常、时序异常和集体异常四种类型，在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上取得SOTA性能（VUS-PR较之前最优提升40.7%），并具备内在异常类型归因能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督多元时间序列异常检测方法大多只针对一到两种异常类型，且可解释性有限。四种结构不同的异常类型（点异常、分布异常、时序异常、集体异常）需要不同的特征表示，亟需一个能同时覆盖所有类型且无需数据集特定调参的统一框架。

Method: CRAFTIIF首先生成K=500个随机解析小波特征（跨Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet四种小波族），每种小波族针对特定异常类型设计。然后构建五个结构化孤立森林（四种单类型IF加一个元IF处理复合异常），每个IF仅在对应类型的特征上训练。使用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值自动校准检测，无需数据集特定调参。每个IF的分支触发直接提供异常类型归因。

Result: 在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上，CRAFTIIF达到平均F1=0.228（全部19个数据集）和F1=0.322（13个可检测数据集）；VUS-PR达到0.463，较之前最优0.329提升40.7%；在所有25种评估方法中排名第一。诊断框架识别出19个数据集中有6个对任何无监督方法均不可检测。消融实验证实自适应阈值（+38% F1）、四分支结构（+20%）和元IF（+23%）均至关重要。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF通过跨分辨率解析小波特征和结构化孤立森林设计，实现了对四种异常类型的全面覆盖与内在归因，是首个在mTSBench上对所有类型均有效的无监督框架。其诊断框架也为评估异常检测的可行性提供了新工具。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [114] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A2D2提出了一个统一的奖励引导微调框架，适用于任意长度离散扩散模型，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现了理论上有保证的收敛到奖励倾斜序列分布，无需目标样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调尚未被探索，现有方法局限于固定长度设置或仅依赖推理时引导。

Method: 推导了联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，建立了去掩码和插入质量作为最小化解码误差的可处理方法，提出了自适应联合解码（AJD）损失函数。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性和准确性上优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: 联合优化插入和去掩码策略的奖励引导微调能有效提升任意长度离散扩散模型的生成性能。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [115] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种调整杯积神经层（Adjusted Cup Product Neural Layer），将杯积与高规范理论的调整项硬编码为神经原语，实现规范不变的读出。理论证明在闭链上输出完全取决于调整系数，且该可观测量为非零二次型，在一阶和二阶规范变换下严格不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理和几何中许多重要可观测量是上链的杯积，但传统神经网络难以保证规范不变性。作者希望通过将杯积与高规范理论调整项结合，设计一种天然具有规范不变性的神经层，以可靠提取拓扑和几何信息。

Method: 提出调整杯积神经层，将杯积运算与高规范理论的调整项硬编码为神经网络的基本原语，使读出器天然满足规范不变性。通过理论分析证明在闭链上输出完全由调整系数决定，且该可观测量是非零二次型。

Result: 理论结果表明：1）在闭链上输出完全取决于调整系数；2）将调整系数设为零则输出完全消失；3）该可观测量是非零二次型；4）在一阶和二阶规范变换下严格不变。

Conclusion: 调整杯积神经层能够设计出规范不变的可观测量，调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源，为物理和几何中的规范不变特征提取提供了新的神经架构方法。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [116] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies是一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重构为未来可观测性推断和数值估计的联合问题，通过双流结构（观测流和数值流）及三个专用模块显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互，在包含自然缺失数据和工业数据的Shadow基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间模型（如Neural ODEs）在推理时隐含假设未来有效观测的时间戳已知，这限制了实际应用价值，因为许多现实系统中不仅需要预测未来数值，还需要判断是否会发生有效观测。

Method: 提出Timeflies双流框架：观测流和数值流，通过三个模块耦合——可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导的依赖建模、联合预测。同时构建Shadow基准并引入观测-数值联合熵（OVJE）指标。

Result: 大量实验表明Timeflies持续优于现有方法，验证了显式建模未来可观测性的重要性。

Conclusion: 时间序列预测中必须显式建模未来可观测性，而不应隐式假设未来观测时间戳已知。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [117] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可模拟过程"框架，证明当学习器能访问数据生成过程的模拟器时，即使数据高度依赖，也能恢复经典独立数据下的VC维泛化保证，并展示了条件采样的统计与计算优势，最终给出一个能同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程的单一算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习理论中的泛化结果大多依赖于数据独立性假设，而对强依赖数据的研究十分有限。为填补这一空白，作者试图探索在数据生成过程可任意复杂且依赖的情况下，最小化泛化所需的假设条件。

Method: 提出"可模拟过程"框架，即学习器可访问一个近似数据分布生成过程的模拟器。基于此框架，利用VC维理论推导泛化误差界，并引入条件采样技术分析统计与计算优势，最后结合时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度设计单一学习算法。

Result: 1) 在可模拟过程框架下，即使数据高度依赖，仍能恢复与经典独立数据设定相同的VC维依赖泛化保证；2) 条件采样在该框架下具有严格的统计与计算优势；3) 单一算法可同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程下的任意VC类，遗憾界由时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 该工作显著拓宽了经典PAC学习模型的适用范围，表明即使在强依赖数据下，只要存在可访问的模拟器，就能获得与独立数据可比的泛化保障，为学习理论提供了新的基础框架。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [118] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCSB框架，通过单纯形约束下的稀疏集成剪枝与概率校准，实现最高96%的集成压缩，同时提升校准性能并保持泛化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bagging集成（如随机森林、Bagged SVM等）给所有基学习器分配均匀投票权重，忽略了不同基学习器在局部区域的差异化能力，导致模型过度自信。

Method: 将集成剪枝与概率校准统一建模为概率单纯形上的优化问题，最小化袋外(OOB)损失；引入凹二次惩罚项解决"L1-单纯形悖论"（L1范数在单纯形上为常数无法产生稀疏性），实现稀疏约束。

Result: 模型无关，最高实现96%的集成压缩，推理速度线性提升，期望校准误差(ECE)显著降低，泛化精度保持或提升。

Conclusion: SCSB是一种数学严谨的后处理框架，能有效压缩Bagging集成并改善概率校准，解决了均匀权重分配带来的过置信问题。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现链式推理（CoT）中存在一个"承诺边界"——模型从临时猜测突然转变为稳定高置信答案的单步转折点，后续推理步骤对最终答案几乎没有影响。通过注意力探针可线性解码答案形成阶段，并利用该信号提前终止推理，平均减少55%的推理长度且性能几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CoT推理是语言模型推理时扩展的主流范式，但个体推理步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚不清楚，研究者希望理解答案在推理轨迹中是如何形成的。

Method: 通过早期退出（early exit）估计每个推理步骤的因果重要性；使用注意力探针（attention probes）从中间推理步骤线性解码答案形成阶段；利用承诺边界信号提前终止推理块。

Result: 推理通常在一个单步中跨越承诺边界，从临时猜测转变为稳定高置信答案；后续步骤是副现象（epiphenomenal），不改变最终答案概率；注意力探针能以高精度解码答案形成阶段，并泛化到未见推理任务；利用该信号可平均减少55%的CoT长度，性能几乎没有下降。

Conclusion: CoT推理中存在可检测的承诺边界，该边界后的推理步骤是冗余的，可在不影响性能的前提下大幅缩短推理过程。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [120] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过恢复子空间维度（k_t）等几何度量，发现持续学习中的灾难性遗忘主要是知识可访问性问题而非信息破坏，遗忘知识仍在低维流形中紧凑可解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"灾难性遗忘即知识破坏"的传统观点，基于Accessibility Collapse框架从几何结构角度探究持续学习中知识可恢复性的本质。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上使用顺序训练的ResNet-18，引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）度量，分析跨10个任务的可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂度，并构建主角度漂移与可恢复性之间的几何模型。

Result: 恢复维度保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0），主角度漂移与可恢复性强相关（r=-0.862），简单几何模型解释82.2%的可恢复性方差，反驳了可恢复性扩散假说。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘本质是可访问性和流形对齐问题而非信息破坏，支持稳定恢复流形假说——遗忘知识在表征重组后仍保持紧凑可解码。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [121] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析多个语言和视觉-语言模型对，发现 On-policy Distillation (OPD) 的更新具有稀疏性和几何集中性：更新稀疏、主要分布在 FFN 层、数值满秩但谱集中，且落在源权重接近零的坐标上。这表明密集的教师监督并未使 OPD 变成普通的密集参数重写，而是保留了 on-policy 后训练的重要几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD 结合了 on-policy 学生轨迹和密集教师监督，已成为重要的后训练方法，但其如何改变模型参数的内在机制尚不清楚。本文旨在通过实证分析揭示 OPD 更新的结构特性。

Method: 在多个语言模型和视觉-语言模型对上，对 OPD 更新进行稀疏性分析和几何分析。稀疏性方面：测量更新幅度、层间分布、FFN 占比；验证仅训练发现的子网络能否恢复全量 OPD 性能；比较 SGD 与 AdamW 优化器。几何方面：分析更新的数值秩和谱集中程度，以及更新方向与源权重奇异子空间和近零坐标的关系。

Result: (1) OPD 更新具有坐标稀疏性，分布在各个层且以 FFN 为主；仅训练发现的子网络可恢复近全量 OPD 性能；但 SGD 在优化器消融中不如 AdamW。(2) OPD 更新数值满秩但谱集中，主要偏离源权重的奇异子空间，且不成比例地落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未将 OPD 变成普通的密集参数重写；OPD 保留了 on-policy 后训练的重要几何特征。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [122] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了截断位置编码（如只使用前k个特征向量或邻接矩阵的前k次幂）在图神经网络中的表达能力，发现不同截断编码在表达能力上有根本区别，截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试，并实验表明混合截断编码优于单一类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管完整版谱编码和游走编码在理论上表达能力等价（介于1-WL和3-WL之间），但实践中常用的是截断变体，这些截断编码的理论性质尚不清楚。

Method: 理论分析截断谱编码、截断游走编码以及k-调和距离等编码的表达能力差异；实验验证混合截断编码在实际数据集上的效果。

Result: 截断条件下不同编码族表达能力存在根本差异；截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试；不同截断谱编码（如k-调和距离）之间的表达能力也有显著区别；混合截断编码优于单一类型。

Conclusion: 截断操作会破坏谱编码和游走编码在理论上的表达能力等价性，实践中应优先考虑混合使用多种截断编码。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Mixed-Categorical Black-Box Optimization via Information-Geometric Bilevel Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12885)
*Marc Ong,Shinichi Shirakawa,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 针对混合类别-连续优化中变量独立假设导致交互性不足的问题，提出双层优化框架：外环优化类别变量，内环条件优化连续变量，结合信息几何随机松弛与热启动策略，在二元-连续域上取得优于现有方法的交互处理能力与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMA-ES类方法假设类别变量与连续变量独立，在强交互场景下性能恶化，亟需一种能显式建模二者依赖关系的混合变量优化方法。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：外层循环优化类别变量，内层循环在给定类别配置下优化连续变量；每层采用信息几何优化下的随机松弛；引入热启动策略，缓存历史最优类别配置以加速内层搜索。

Result: 在二元-连续域基准测试上，所提方法在交互处理能力上超越现有最优方法，同时计算效率更高。

Conclusion: 双层优化框架能有效捕捉类别-连续变量间的强交互，热启动策略缓解了双层优化的高计算成本，在混合变量黑箱优化中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Mixed categorical-continuous optimization arises in many practical domains, yet remains challenging. In the black-box setting, evolution strategy-based approaches have shown promise in extending the efficiency and robustness of the CMA-ES to mixed-variable spaces. However, these methods exhibit worsened performance when strong categorical-continuous interactions are present, as their underlying search distributions assume independence between categorical and continuous variables. To address this limitation, we propose a bilevel optimization framework that explicitly captures such interactions by optimizing over categorical variables in an outer loop, and over continuous variables conditioned on each categorical configuration in an inner loop. We formulate each level of the bilevel problem as a stochastic relaxation under information-geometric optimization. To mitigate the high computational cost inherent to bilevel optimization, we introduce a warm-starting strategy that accelerates the lower-level search by selecting the best among multiple cached configurations and updating the cache after each iteration. Experimental results on binary-continuous domain demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches in interaction-handling capability while also being more computationally efficient across benchmarks encompassing both previously reported and newly proposed types of interaction.

</details>


### [124] [Multi-Objective Coevolution of Prompts and Templates for Circuit Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13089)
*Martin Tomasovic,Lukas Sekanina*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用现成大语言模型（LLM）的协同进化算法，无需领域特定训练即可自动设计优化的8位近似乘法器，在误差-面积权衡上优于EvoApproxLib库中的高度优化电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似乘法器通过放宽计算精度来提升能效、降低延迟和减少硅面积，适用于神经网络等容错应用。现有设计方法需要领域专家知识或大量人工调优，缺乏自动化高效设计手段。

Method: 提出一种协同进化算法，同时进化候选项电路群体和提示模板群体，利用现成LLM（无需领域特定训练）驱动电路修改，自动生成优化的8位近似乘法器。

Result: 针对多个目标设计目标的实验结果表明，所提方法发现的近似乘法器在误差-面积权衡上优于EvoApproxLib库中高度优化的电路。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的协同进化算法能够有效自动化设计高性能近似乘法器，无需领域特定训练即可取得优于现有库的结果，为近似计算电路设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Approximate multipliers deliberately relax computational accuracy to achieve gains in power efficiency, latency, and silicon area, which makes them well-suited for error-resilient applications such as neural networks. In this work, we introduce a co-evolutionary algorithm that leverages an off-the-shelf large language model (LLM) without requiring domain-specific training to automate the design of optimized 8-bit approximate multipliers. The approach simultaneously evolves a population of candidate circuits and a population of prompt templates that steer LLM-driven modifications. Experimental results for several target design objectives demonstrate that the proposed method discovers approximate multipliers with improved error-area trade-offs compared to highly optimized circuits from the EvoApproxLib library.

</details>


### [125] [Runtime Analysis of the $(μ+ 1)$-ES in a Homogenous Progress Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13323)
*Johannes Lengler,Raghu Raman Ravi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化模型来研究进化策略(ES)在通用问题中的适应度进展。该模型绕过底层适应度景观，假设任何个体的变异产生的后代相对于父代的适应度由不变分布Z决定。作者严格分析了连续稳态(μ+1)-ES在此模型中的期望增长率R_μ，通过构建修改过程来夹逼原始过程，给出了紧界。当Z=N(-δ,1)且μ≤e^δ时，证明了R_μ = (log^{1+o(1)} μ/μ) R_1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化策略(ES)的理论分析通常受限于复杂的适应度景观，难以对通用问题进行精确建模。特别是(μ+1)-ES这类稳态选择策略存在世代重叠，导致存活父代间复杂的数学依赖关系，使得分析更加困难。作者希望通过构建一个简化的不变分布模型来绕过具体的适应度景观，从而在保证分析可处理性的同时，捕捉ES优化过程的核心特征。

Method: 提出一种简化模型：假设个体变异后，后代相对于父代的适应度由不变分布Z（如均值偏移高斯分布）决定，该模型近似算法远离全局最优时的情况。为分析(μ+1)-ES的期望增长率R_μ，作者构建了修改后的过程，其增长率可证明地夹逼原始过程的增长率。这些修改后的过程更易于分析，同时仍足够接近真实过程，从而给出R_μ的紧界。

Result: 当Z=N(-δ,1)（均值偏移高斯分布）且μ≤e^δ时，证明了(μ+1)-ES的期望增长率R_μ = (log^{1+o(1)} μ/μ) R_1。

Conclusion: 本文提出的简化模型为分析ES在通用问题中的优化过程提供了新的理论框架，尤其适用于精确适应度函数难以建模的场景。通过夹逼技术成功分析了(μ+1)-ES的增长率，揭示了种群规模μ与增长率之间的定量关系。

Abstract: We introduce a new simple model to study the fitness progress of Evolution Strategies (ES) in generic problems. In this model, we bypass the underlying fitness landscape and assume that the mutation of any individual produces an offspring whose fitness relative to the parent is given by an invariant distribution $Z$, such as a mean-shifted Gaussian. This serves as a prototypical model for the optimisation landscape when an evolution algorithm operates far from the global optimum. This simple model can be used to approximate the optimisation process for problems where it is intractable to model the exact fitness function, including tasks such as hyperparameter tuning in machine learning models.
  We rigorously analyse the expected growth rate $\mathcal{R}_μ$ of the continuous steady-state $(μ+1)$-ES in this model. Unlike comma-selection strategies, the steady-state $(μ+1)$-ES maintains overlapping generations, introducing complex mathematical dependencies among surviving parents that make it harder to analyse. We give a general technique to analyse the the $(μ+ 1)$-ES by constructing modified processes whose growth rates provably sandwich that of the original process. These modified processes are then easier to analyse but still close enough to the true process to give a tight bound on the expected growth rate. When $Z = \mathcal{N}(-δ, 1)$ and $μ\le e^δ$, we show that $\mathcal{R}_μ = \frac{\log^{1 + o(1)} μ}μ \mathcal{R}_1$.

</details>


### [126] [Improved Runtime Bound for the $(μ+ 1)$ EA on BinVal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13344)
*Joris Belder,Johannes Lengler,Raghu Raman Ravi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了(μ+1) EA在Binary Value函数上的运行时间，证明了当μ = o(n/log n)时，最多需要O(μ log μ · n log n)次函数评估即可找到最优解，显著改进了之前O(μ^5 n log(n/μ^4))的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进(μ+1) EA在Binary Value函数上的已知上界，缩小其与OneMax函数上的性能差距。

Method: 通过更精细的分析方法，针对多种变异算子（包括标准位变异）推导出更紧的运行时间上界。

Result: 当μ = o(n/log n)时，(μ+1) EA在BinVal上的运行时间上界为O(μ log μ · n log n)，比之前O(μ^5 n log(n/μ^4))的结果有显著改进。

Conclusion: (μ+1) EA在BinVal上最多比在OneMax上慢O(log μ · log n)倍，表明两者的性能差距很小。

Abstract: We study the $(μ+1)$ EA on the Binary Value function BinVal. We show that it needs at most $O(μ\log μ\cdot n \log n)$ function evaluations to find the optimum when $μ= o(n/\log n)$. This substantially improves upon the recent upper bound of $O(μ^5 n \log(n/μ^4))$ by Krejca, Neumann and Witt. Our results hold for several mutation operators including standard bit mutation. In particular, our bound implies that the $(μ+1)$ EA is at most a factor $O(\log μ\cdot \log n)$ slower on BinVal than on OneMax.

</details>


### [127] [The $(1 + 1)$-EA in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13360)
*Georg Hasebe,Johannes Lengler,Raghu Raman Ravi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了(1+1)-EA在动态线性环境中的优化行为，针对两种动态权重模型（指数增长权重和均匀分布权重），发现了突变率参数χ的尖锐阈值：低于阈值时优化时间为O(n log n)，高于阈值时变为2^{Ω(n)}。对于指数权重模型，还发现了第二个阈值，量化了优化过程停滞的距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解进化算法在动态环境中的行为，特别是突变率如何影响算法在动态线性问题上的优化效率，并验证和量化先前的经验发现。

Method: 对(1+1)-EA在两种动态线性权重模型（Dynamic Binary Value问题使用指数增长权重的随机排列，以及Uniform权重模型使用Unif(0,1)独立采样）进行理论分析，研究突变参数χ对优化时间的影响。

Result: 发现了突变率参数χ的尖锐阈值：当χ低于阈值时，期望优化时间为O(n log n)；高于阈值时，运行时间变为指数级2^{Ω(n)}。对于Dynamic Binary Value问题，还发现了第二个阈值，量化了优化过程停滞的距离。

Conclusion: 进化算法在动态线性环境中存在突变率的临界阈值，该阈值决定了算法能否高效优化。研究结果量化并验证了先前的经验发现。

Abstract: We study the $(1 + 1)$-EA in dynamic linear environments, where in every generation selection is performed with respect to a freshly sampled linear function with positive weights. We consider the Dynamic Binary Value problem, where each generation uses a uniformly random permutation of $1,2,4,\dots,2^{n-1}$, and a Uniform weight variant, where the weights are drawn independently from $\mathrm{Unif}(0,1)$. Both of them have recently been integrated into the IOHprofiler platform and empirically studied.
  For both models we prove a sharp threshold in the mutation parameter $χ$ for mutation rate $χ/n$. Below the threshold, the expected optimisation time is $\mathcal{O}(n\log n)$, whereas above it the runtime becomes $2^{Ω(n)}$.
  For the Dynamic Binary Value problem in the exponential regime, we also quantify at what distance from the optimum the optimisation process stagnates. We show that there is a second threshold: a distance that is efficiently reached, but reaching any smaller distance takes exponential time. This quantifies and proves previous empirical findings.

</details>


### [128] [Adaptive-Frequency Resonate-and-Fire Neurons for Spectral Estimation of Streaming Radar Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13516)
*Stefano Chiavazza,Sen Yuan,Marc Geilen,Francesco Fioranelli,Federico Corradi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自适应谐振-放电（ARF）神经元的神经形态信号处理方法，用于FMCW雷达的距离和速度估计。该方法以逐样本方式运行，内存需求仅与跟踪目标数成正比，而非信号长度，在资源受限的边缘雷达应用中相比传统FFT方法具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FMCW雷达依赖FFT等傅里叶方法进行距离和速度估计，虽然计算高效，但需要存储和处理大块数据，在内存受限或低延迟应用中成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于自适应谐振-放电（ARF）神经元的离散时间动力系统模型。每个神经元的内部频率可动态调整以匹配输入雷达信号中的主导频率分量，通过逐样本处理直接估计目标距离和速度，无需计算完整频谱；引入反馈机制使多个神经元能够锁定多目标场景中的不同频率分量。

Result: 在仿真和实验数据上的结果表明，该方法能够成功跟踪多个目标。相比传统FFT方法，所提方法的内存使用量与跟踪目标数成正比，显著降低。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于资源受限和边缘端的雷达应用，为FMCW雷达信号处理提供了一种低内存占用的神经形态替代方案。

Abstract: Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) radar systems traditionally rely on Fourier-based methods, such as the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT), to estimate target range and velocity. While computationally efficient, these approaches require storing and processing large blocks of data, which can become a bottleneck in memory-constrained or low-latency applications. In this work, we propose a neuromorphic-inspired signal processing method based on adaptive resonate-and-fire (ARF) neurons formulated as a discrete-time dynamical system. Each neuron dynamically adjusts its internal frequency to match dominant frequency components of the input radar signal, enabling direct estimation of target ranges and velocities without computing the full frequency spectrum. The proposed model operates in a sample-by-sample fashion, resulting in memory requirements that scale with the number of tracked targets rather than the signal length. A feedback mechanism is also introduced to enable multiple neurons to lock on distinct frequency components in multi-target cases. Results on simulated and experimental data demonstrate that the method can successfully track multiple targets. Compared to conventional FFT-based approaches, the proposed method offers reduced memory usage proportional only to the number of tracked targets, making it suitable for resource-constrained and edge-based radar applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Bounding Boxes as Goals: Language-Conditioned Grasping via Neuro-Symbolic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12910)
*Allison Andreyev,Landon Eum,Nestor Tiglao,Romel Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRASP是一个利用预训练VLM将自然语言查询转化为神经符号目标状态、无需额外训练即可实现开放词汇桌面操作任务的开源框架，在90次真实机器人试验中达到73.3%的整体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM用于机器人任务与运动规划时，计算开销大或依赖大量 demonstrations 训练，缺乏轻量级、零微调的开放词汇操作方案。

Method: 利用预训练VLM将自然语言查询翻译为神经符号目标状态，通过边界框检测管线进行物理世界接地，无需固定颜色列表或硬编码坐标，即可解释"顶层架子"等抽象空间概念。

Result: 在90次真实机器人试验中，三个难度级别整体成功率达73.3%，无需任务特定训练。

Conclusion: GRASP证明了利用预训练VLM实现零微调、开放词汇桌面操作的可行性，为机器人理解抽象空间概念并实时执行任务提供了有效途径。

Abstract: For robotics to be effectively integrated into household or industrial environments, machines must adapt to natural-language prompts in real time. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled zero-shot generalization in robot task and motion planning (TAMP), current state-of-the-art approaches often remain computationally "heavyweight" or require extensive training on thousands of demonstrations. We present GRASP (Grounded Reasoning and Symbolic Planning), a framework designed as a step toward open-vocabulary tabletop manipulation. Our approach leverages a pretrained VLM to translate natural-language queries into neuro-symbolic goal states, grounded in the physical world via a bounding-box detection pipeline. Unlike methods that rely on fixed color lists or hard-coded coordinates, GRASP enables robots to interpret abstract spatial concepts such as "top shelf" and execute tasks without additional fine-tuning. We achieve 73.3% overall success across 90 real-robot trials at three difficulty levels, requiring no task-specific training.

</details>


### [130] [Learning to Assist: Collaborative VLAs for Implicit Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12475)
*Leo Xu,Letian Li,Alex Cuellar,Michael Hagenow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于模仿学习的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在人机协作（HRC）中的应用，发现动作分块策略存在"示范动作泄漏"问题（即动作块跨越隐式任务转换边界导致过早的辅助行为），并提出了一种推理时引导方法来缓解该问题。通过16人用户研究验证，该方法在长时域协作任务中能减少过早辅助，提升协作效率并降低失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRC系统多依赖手工设计的流程，限制了其可扩展性和对新任务的灵活性。本文旨在探索端到端训练的VLA模型能否支持协作操作，并识别影响其实世界性能的关键因素。

Method: 评估两种最先进的VLA模型，识别出动作分块策略在隐式HRC中的"示范动作泄漏"故障模式；提出一种推理时引导方法来修正错误的辅助行为；通过16人用户研究在长时域协作装配任务上进行验证。

Result: "示范动作泄漏"问题随执行时域增长而加剧，在真实协作VLA系统中确实存在（如机器人过早递工具）。所提引导方法能在保持策略性能的同时缓解错误的辅助行为，在长时域协作中比短时域策略更快且失败率更低。

Conclusion: 端到端训练的VLA模型可以支持协作操作，但动作分块策略存在跨任务边界泄漏的问题；推理时引导是一种有效的缓解手段，能够使策略在更长的执行时域下保持良好性能。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) combines the complementary strengths of humans and robots to improve task efficiency. However, many existing collaborative systems rely on hand-engineered pipelines, limiting their scalability and flexibility for new tasks. In this work, we show that models trained end-to-end with imitation learning, specifically vision-language-action (VLA) models, can support collaborative manipulation, and characterize the key factors affecting their real-world performance. We evaluate two state-of-the-art models and identify a failure mode of action-chunking policies in implicit HRC, where demonstration action leakage (i.e., action chunks crossing latent task transitions) can cause premature assistive behavior. We find that this issue increases with longer execution horizons and occurs in real-world collaborative VLA systems, such as when a robot attempts to hand over a tool before the person is ready. We propose an inference-time steering method to mitigate these erroneous assistive actions while preserving policy performance. Finally, through a 16-participant user study on a long-horizon collaborative assembly task, we show that steering enables a longer execution horizon while mitigating premature assistance, leading to faster collaboration and fewer failures compared to a shorter-horizon policy.

</details>


### [131] [Multi-Modal Multi-Agent Robotic Cognitive Alignment enabled by Non-Invasive Consumer Brain Computer Interfaces: A Proof of Concept Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13190)
*Nataliya Kosmyna,Liz Jenkins,Anoop K. Sinha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过消费级脑机接口（BCI）实时监测人类脑电（EEG）频谱功率，实现多智能体系统在人高认知负荷时自动延迟通信的“认知对齐”框架，初步验证了将实时信号处理、大语言模型（LLM）与实体机器人结合构建非侵入式认知感知多智能体系统的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机交互方法忽视人类内部认知状态，主动式多智能体系统常在不当时机打断用户，导致认知过载和任务绩效下降。因此需要一种能在用户高认知负荷时智能延迟通信的框架。

Method: 设计并实现闭环架构：利用消费级BCI持续监测EEG频谱带功率以检测用户高投入状态；构建基于HTTP信号机制的投入驱动管线，在高认知负荷时主智能体将感官输入和音频输出置入保持状态，由次智能体在后台处理复杂委托任务；当认知负荷恢复基线后，主智能体释放排队信息。

Result: 初步结果验证了将实时信号处理、LLM和物理机器人实体相结合，创建认知感知、非侵入式多智能体系统的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建认知对齐的多智能体交互提供了有效路径，通过脑电监测实现上下文感知的通信延迟，有望提升人机协作的自然性与任务效率。

Abstract: While non-verbal behaviors and expressive movements are essential for natural human-robot interaction, existing methods often overlook a crucial element: the human's internal cognitive state. Frequently, proactive multi-agent systems can interrupt humans at inopportune moments, leading to cognitive overload and decreased task performance. This paper introduces a framework for generating "cognitively aligned" multi-agent interactions, enhancing the ability of robotic systems to contextually defer communications to the user of an agent system during moments of high human mental workload and engagement. We present the design and implementation of a closed-loop architecture that explores the interplay between autonomous task execution and real-time neurophysiological focus. Using a consumer-grade Brain-Computer Interface (BCI), our approach continuously monitors Electroencephalography (EEG) spectral band powers while a human performs an engagement-inducing task. We propose an engagement-driven pipeline where an HTTP-based signaling mechanism places a primary agent's sensory inputs and audio outputs into a holding state upon detecting high engagement. This allows secondary agents to seamlessly process complex, delegated tasks in the background. Once the human's cognitive state returns to a lower cognitive load baseline, the primary agent releases the queued agent message. Our preliminary results demonstrate the feasibility of leveraging real-time signal processing, Large Language Models (LLMs), and physical robotic embodiments to create cognitively-aware, non-intrusive multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [132] [Trajectory-Level Redirection Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12978)
*Gokul Puthumanaillam,Vardhan Dongre,Pranay Thangeda,Hooshang Nayyeri,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的轨迹级攻击：攻击者可以构造一个看似保留原始指令的对抗性提示，实际上却将机器人执行结果重定向到攻击者指定的目标。作者形式化定义了"命令保留轨迹重定向"威胁模型，并提出基于策略回滚的提示搜索方法，在仿真和硬件实验中验证了该漏洞的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA攻击仅研究诱导特定低级动作或使动作在不同图像下持续存在的对抗性提示，但缺乏对轨迹级失效模式的关注——即提示表面上仍指定原任务，但最终物理结果被重定向。

Method: 形式化定义"命令保留轨迹重定向"（command-preserving trajectory redirection）威胁模型：攻击者在回合前选择一个提示，策略和环境组件保持不变，提示需接近良性指令且省略目标词和纠正性语言。提出on-policy提示搜索方法，利用回滚（rollouts）发现满足约束的对抗性扰动。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验表明，近乎良性的提示扰动能够将VLA策略的回滚重定向到攻击者指定的目标，证明存在轨迹级指令基础漏洞。

Conclusion: 文本指令即使看起来保留了原始命令意图，仍可能被攻击者利用来控制机器人的最终物理结果，揭示了VLA策略在指令语义理解上的安全隐患。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies bring natural language into closed-loop robot control, enabling robots to execute manipulation tasks directly from text instructions. The same interface gives text a recurring role in control because the prompt is reused at every replanning step, and each prompt-conditioned action changes the future observations on which the policy acts. Existing VLA attacks study adversarial prompts that elicit targeted low-level actions or make such actions persist across changing images. We identify a stronger trajectory-level failure mode: a prompt that still $\textit{appears}$ to specify the intended task but redirects the final physical outcome. We mathematically formalize this setting as $\textit{command-preserving trajectory redirection}$, a prompt-only threat model in which the attacker chooses one prompt before the episode, all policy and environment components remain fixed, and the prompt must stay close to the benign instruction while omitting target words and correction language. To find such prompts, we introduce an on-policy prompt search method that uses rollouts to discover perturbations whose closed-loop behavior tracks a target task while satisfying the command-preserving constraints. Experiments in simulation and on hardware show that near-benign prompt perturbations can redirect VLA rollouts to attacker-specified targets. These results expose a trajectory-level vulnerability in VLA instruction grounding: text that appears to preserve the intended command can still give an adversary control over the robot's final physical outcome. Project website: https://vla-redirection-attack.github.io/

</details>


### [133] [Action-Effect Memory Pretraining for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12499)
*Yijing Zhou,Qiwei Liang,Sitong Zhuang,Jiaxi Li,Xianpeng Wang,Boyang Cai,Yunyang Mo,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AEM是一个面向机器人操作的行动-效应记忆预训练框架，通过将视觉与动作特征交织并进行掩码建模来学习紧凑的时间表征，在仿真和真实场景中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人表征预训练方法主要关注单帧视觉编码，忽视了操作任务的时间本质——在部分可观测性下，当前观测单独往往不足以做出决策。

Method: 将视觉与动作特征交织，应用掩码建模从残缺历史中恢复缺失内容；采用Mamba编码最终视觉token作为紧凑历史表征（单向量时间瓶颈），用于解码和下流控制。

Result: 结合Diffusion Policy和Flow Policy，在仿真和真实场景中一致提升操作性能，在干净场景、杂乱/随机场景及非马尔可夫任务中均优于基线；消融实验表明历史感知预训练优于单帧预训练和直接帧堆叠，同时降低推理延迟和计算成本。

Conclusion: AEM通过行动-效应记忆预训练有效捕捉操作任务中的时间依赖关系，为机器人学习提供了高效且性能优越的紧凑时序表征框架。

Abstract: We present AEM, an Action-Effect Memory pretraining framework for robot manipulation that learns compact temporal representations from vision-action history. Unlike prior robot representation pretraining methods that mainly focus on single-frame visual encoding, AEM targets the temporal nature of manipulation, where the current observation alone is often insufficient under partial observability. AEM models manipulation as an action-driven interaction process by interleaving visual and action features and applying masked modeling to recover missing content from incomplete histories, thereby learning action-conditioned state evolution. The Mamba-encoded output of the final vision token is used as a compact history representation, serving as the global context for decoding and downstream control. This design preserves a single-vector temporal bottleneck while keeping inference efficient. We evaluate AEM with Diffusion Policy and Flow Policy. AEM consistently improves manipulation performance in both simulation and real-world settings, outperforming baselines across clean scenes, cluttered and random scenes, and non-Markovian tasks. Ablation studies further show that history-aware pretraining surpasses single-frame pretraining and direct frame stacking, while reducing inference latency and computational cost.

</details>


### [134] [MCR-Bionic Hand: Anatomical Structural Priors for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13601)
*Haosen Yang,Guowu Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出结构智能框架，包含结构先验生成（腕指连动、肌腱走行、伸肌腱帽）和肌肉介导调节（外在肌、蚓状肌、骨间肌），并基于此开发MCR-Bionic仿生手，通过1:1肌肉骨骼仿生实现低维姿态生成与精细接触后调节，证明解剖仿生学的价值在于识别执行控制功能的人手结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示人手灵巧性部分编码于骨骼-韧带-肌腱-腱膜-内在肌的物理结构中，而非仅由高维主动控制系统决定，从而为仿生手设计提供新的结构智能理论框架。

Method: 提出结构智能框架（结构先验生成+肌肉介导调节），并基于此开发1:1肌肉骨骼仿生手MCR-Bionic，集成两排八腕骨、交叉腕肌腱、解剖屈肌腱走行、掌板/侧副韧带约束、伸肌腱帽及内在肌通路，通过功能演示和几何力学模型验证。

Result: 腕姿诱导多关节预塑形，伸肌腱帽将PIP姿态映射为耦合的DIP响应，内在肌+通路调节抓取后的远端稳定性和指尖作用方向；在硬币旋转、笔传递、硬币翻转、方块操作等接触密集型任务中实现低维状态生成与精细接触后调节的结合。

Conclusion: 解剖仿生学的价值不在于视觉相似性，而在于识别人手结构中执行部分控制功能的组件，为简化仿生手控制提供结构层面的解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands are usually formulated as high dimensional active control systems governed by degrees of freedom, actuation, and algorithms. Human hand dexterity, however, is partly encoded in the physical architecture of bones, ligaments, tendons, aponeuroses, and intrinsic muscles. This work describes that contribution as two linked forms of structural intelligence: structural prior generation, in which wrist to finger tenodesis, FDS/FDP routing, and the dorsal extensor hood transform low dimensional posture inputs into default grasp configurations and PIP to DIP coordination; and muscle mediated modulation, in which extrinsic muscles, lumbricals, and interossei regulate MCP posture, distal stability, fingertip force paths, and contact states around that default state.
  Based on this framework, MCR-Bionic Hand is developed as a 1:1 musculoskeletal biomimetic hand integrating a two row eight bone wrist, cross wrist tendons, anatomical flexor routing, volar plate and collateral ligament constraints, the dorsal extensor hood, and intrinsic muscle pathways within one body. Functional demonstrations and geometric mechanical models show that wrist posture induces multi joint pre shaping, the extensor hood maps PIP posture to a coupled DIP response, and intrinsic plus pathways modulate distal stability and fingertip action direction after grasp formation. Contact rich tasks, including coin rotation, pen transfer, dorsal coin flipping, and cube manipulation, show that MCR-Bionic links low dimensional state generation with fine post contact modulation. These results suggest that anatomical biomimetics is valuable not for visual similarity, but for identifying human hand structures that perform part of control.

</details>


### [135] [Foresight: Iterative Reasoning About Clues that Matter for Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12550)
*Arthur Zhang,Carl Qi,Donne Su,Xiangyun Meng,Amy Zhang,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Foresight框架，利用微调后的视觉-语言模型(VLM)在测试时交替生成图像空间运动计划并进行批判，通过人类反馈学习奖励模型并强化学习后训练VLM，实现开放世界无地图导航。在离线评估和6个真实环境中，任务成功率平均提升37%，每次任务干预减少52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界无地图导航方法受限于依赖已知导航因素和封闭集因子类别，或者在运动规划前识别线索而遗漏规划相关线索。需要一种能发现新颖指令相关线索、自适应关注重要线索及其对运动规划影响的方法。

Method: Foresight框架：1) 微调VLM交替生成图像空间运动计划并基于语言目标和视觉上下文进行批判；2) 后续计划基于先前批判条件，实现执行前迭代运动优化；3) 从人类反馈学习奖励模型，使用强化学习在计划-批判循环中对VLM进行后训练。

Result: 在离线评估和6个真实环境中，相比最先进的测试时推理和基础模型基线，Foresight将平均任务成功率提升37%，每次任务干预减少52%，并在Jetson AGX Orin上实时运行。

Conclusion: Foresight证明了通过测试时推理循环和人类反馈强化学习微调VLM，能有效解决开放世界无地图导航中的目标欠指定和线索推断问题，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Open-world mapless navigation from sparse language instructions requires resolving underspecified goals and inferring which environmental cues are relevant for reaching the goal. For instance, reaching an out-of-view destination may require interpreting ramps, signs, or detours that reveal where to go or which route to take. Prior works are limited by their reliance on known navigation factors and closed-set factor categories, or identify cues before motion planning and miss plan-dependent cues. We argue that pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can discover novel instruction-relevant cues, but require adaptation to focus on which cues matter and how they should influence motion planning. We realize these ideas in Foresight, a test-time framework in which a finetuned VLM alternates between proposing image-space motion plans and critiquing them using the language goal and visual context. Subsequent plans are conditioned on prior critiques, enabling iterative motion refinement before execution. To align plan critiques and refinements with open-set behavior preferences, we learn a reward model from human feedback and use it to post-train the VLM with reinforcement learning in the plan-critique loop. In offline evaluations and 6 real-world environments, Foresight improves average task success by 37% and reduces interventions per mission by 52% relative to state-of-the-art test-time reasoning and foundation-model baselines, while running in real-time on a Jetson AGX Orin. We will release code, data, and training details to support future work on test-time reasoning for robot motion refinement. Additional videos at: https://amrl.cs.utexas.edu/foresight

</details>


### [136] [G-MAPP: GPU-accelerated Multi-Agent Planning and Perception for Reactive Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12579)
*Tanmay Bishnoi,Riddhiman Laha,Tobias Löw,Jose Alex Chandy,Luis F. C. Figueredo,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU加速的机器人反应式运动生成框架，通过加速世界建模和向量场规划，实现了在动态杂乱环境中的实时准全局轨迹规划，在7自由度Franka Emika机器人上验证了最高5倍速度提升和有效避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么为静态场景生成全局轨迹，要么采用对环境做出保守假设的模型，主要瓶颈在于高保真环境下的运行时性能需求以及感知与规划模块之间的时间集成。

Method: 提出一个利用GPU加速世界建模和基于向量场的规划的框架，实现更快的并行状态探索以实现准全局轨迹规划，并通过紧耦合感知-动作循环实现实时反应式运动生成。

Result: GPU加速版本相比CPU版本最高实现5倍速度提升，在7自由度Franka Emika机器人的真实物理场景中成功实现了避障。

Conclusion: 该框架在不牺牲运行时性能和世界表示质量的前提下，通过GPU加速实现了更紧密的感知-规划耦合，有效解决了动态杂乱环境中的反应式运动生成问题。

Abstract: Reactive motion generation in unstructured environments remains an open challenge in robotics. Due to the computational complexity of collision-free motion generation, existing methods either generate global trajectories for static scenarios, or employ models that make conservative assumptions about the environment. This paper identifies the primary bottleneck as the runtime performance demand of planning on high-fidelity environments, and the temporal integration between the perception and planning modules. Therefore, we propose a framework that does not compromise on runtime performance and world representations for perception and planning by accelerating world modeling and vector-field based planning using the GPU. This allows us to achieve faster parallel state exploration for quasi-global trajectory planning, and tighter coupling of the perception-action loop in real-time for dynamic cluttered environments with off-the-shelf depth sensors. We quantitatively evaluate the computation-time and success rate differences for the CPU and GPU versions of our planner, and perform qualitative evaluations of our coupled framework using real-world experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Emika robot. Experimental results demonstrate that our GPU-based framework achieves up to a 5x speedup over the CPU version and successfully avoids collisions across both trivial and challenging physical world scenarios.

</details>


### [137] [From Imitation to Alignment: Human-Preference Flow Policies for Long-Horizon Sidewalk Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12603)
*Honglin He,Zhizheng Liu,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPilot是一种基于单目RGB相机的无地图导航策略，通过锚定流匹配(action representation)进行预训练，并结合人类偏好学习微调，实现了42%仿真成功率和66%路线完成率，在真实世界中将干预率降低40.0%，将接近干预率降低52.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主长时域人行道导航对微出行应用至关重要，但现有模仿学习方法存在复合误差、缺乏社交合规性和反事实推理能力，且需要轻量级感知栈。

Method: 提出锚定流匹配(anchored flow matching)作为动作表征，在大规模机器人车队数据上预训练策略；设计人机协作偏好学习方案，利用少量人类干预数据进行策略微调，增强反事实推理和社交合规性。

Result: 仿真环境：42%成功率、66%路线完成率；真实世界：干预率降低40.0%，接近干预率降低52.1%。

Conclusion: FlowPilot证明了仅用单目RGB相机的轻量级方案即可实现鲁棒高效的长时域人行道导航，偏好学习有效弥合了模仿学习与行为对齐之间的差距。

Abstract: Autonomous long-horizon sidewalk navigation is essential for micro-mobility applications such as robotic food delivery and assistive electronic wheelchairs. Unlike autonomous driving on the road, long-horizon sidewalk navigation requires precise maneuvering through unpredictable sidewalk terrains and pedestrians, with a lightweight perception stack as minimal as a single monocular RGB camera. While imitation learning (IL) from demonstrations offers a practical solution, the resulting autopilot policy often suffers from compounding errors, a lack of social compliance on sidewalks, and deficiencies in counterfactual reasoning to handle complex situations. To address these challenges, we introduce FlowPilot, a mapless navigation policy that achieves robust and efficient long-horizon navigation performance using only a monocular RGB camera. We first propose to use anchored flow matching as an action representation for policy pre-training on large-scale robot fleet data and to capture the diverse, complex, multimodal distribution of sidewalk navigation behaviors. To bridge the gap between imitation and alignment, we further design a human-in-the-loop preference learning scheme to tune the policy on a small amount of human intervention data. It strengthens the model's counterfactual reasoning and social compliance on sidewalks. We evaluate FlowPilot through extensive simulation and real-world experiments in diverse sidewalk environments. FlowPilot achieves 42% success rate and 66% route completion in simulation, while FlowPilot-HP further improves real-world robustness and social compliance, reducing IR by 40.0% and NIR by 52.1% relative to the base model.

</details>


### [138] [EgoEngine: From Egocentric Human Videos to High-Fidelity Dexterous Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12604)
*Yangcen Liu,Shuo Cheng,Xinchen Yin,Woo Chul Shin,Alfred Cueva,Yiran Yang,Zhenyang Chen,Chuye Zhang,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoEngine是一个将第一人称人类操作视频转化为高保真机器人数据的框架，通过桥接视觉差异和动作差异，首次实现从人类视频零样本学习灵巧操作策略，无需真实机器人演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作受限于大规模机器人演示数据的采集成本，而第一人称人类视频提供了丰富的操作行为来源，但直接用于机器人学习需要解决人类与机器人之间的视觉差异和动作差异两大鸿沟。

Method: 提出EgoEngine框架，输入第一人称RGB视频，输出：(i) 用机器人替换人类的高保真观测视频，保持场景上下文和时间对齐；(ii) 在可行性约束下与任务对齐的可执行机器人动作轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，EgoEngine实现了将人类视频可扩展地转化为机器人数据，并首次展示了从第一人称人类视频零样本学习视触觉灵巧操作策略，无需真实机器人演示。

Conclusion: EgoEngine有效桥接了人类演示与机器人执行之间的视觉和动作鸿沟，为利用大规模人类视频数据驱动灵巧操作学习提供了一种可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is limited by the cost of collecting large-scale robot demonstrations. Egocentric human videos offer a scalable source of diverse manipulation behaviors, but directly using them for robot learning requires bridging two gaps: the visual gap between human and robot observations, and the action gap between human motion and robot-executable action. We propose EgoEngine, a scalable framework for transforming egocentric human manipulation videos into high-fidelity robot data. Given an egocentric RGB video, EgoEngine produces: (i) a high-fidelity robot observation video replacing human with robot while preserving scene context and temporal alignment, and (ii) a task-aligned, executable robot action trajectory under feasibility constraints. Experiments in simulation and on real robots show that EgoEngine enables scalable conversion of human videos into robot data and, to our knowledge, demonstrates the first zero-shot visuomotor dexterous policy learning from egocentric human videos without real-robot demonstrations. Project website: https://egoengine.github.io.

</details>


### [139] [DARRMS -- An Efficient Algorithm for Dynamic Attention Radius in Resource-Constrained Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12614)
*Benjamin Alcorn,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过限制智能体观测范围（注意力半径）来降低计算资源需求的新算法，在不显著牺牲性能的前提下提升多智能体系统的协调性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在机器人、网络安全和自动驾驶等领域应用广泛，但传统决策框架假设完全可观测和无限计算能力，与现实中的资源受限环境不符，亟需轻量级高效算法。

Method: 引入注意力半径机制，让智能体主动限制观测范围，忽略环境中对行动规划不必要的部分，同时联合优化注意力半径和决策策略。

Result: 理论分析和实验验证均表明，自适应观测方法能在资源受限系统中有效提升系统性能，并保持稳健的决策能力。

Conclusion: 通过主动限制观测范围降低计算负载，可在不显著牺牲其他性能指标的情况下，提高多智能体系统的协调性和可扩展性，适合实际资源受限场景。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are integral tools for various domains such as robotics, cybersecurity, and autonomous vehicle planning. These types of systems often have constraints on the computational resources, leading to a need for efficient lightweight algorithms. Traditional decision making frameworks often assume ideal conditions, such as full observability and unlimited computational capacity, which do not align with real-world challenges. In this paper, we introduce a new algorithm that allows for reduced demand on computational resources without a large cost of other performance metrics. Agents will limit their observability to some attention radius, which intentionally allows them to ignore parts of the environment that might be unnecessary for action planning. By optimizing both the attention radius and decision-making, our approach enhances coordination and scalability in uncertain environments. Through both theoretical analysis and empirical validation, we demonstrate the effectiveness of adaptive observation in improving system performance and maintaining robust decision-making strategies in resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [140] [EWAM: An Enhanced World Action Model for Closed-Loop Online Adaptation in Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12690)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出增强型世界行动模型(EWAM)，一种基于冻结Cosmos3骨干网络的闭环在线自适应架构。通过四个轻量级神经层（经验记忆层、异常检测层、策略路由层、动作修正层）的推理时协同推理机制，在零样本协议下减少新任务布局的部署数据需求，无需微调骨干网络或引入额外演示集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少在零样本任务协议下适应新任务布局所需的额外部署数据量，避免对预训练骨干网络进行微调或引入额外任务特定演示集。
