The system introduces two conceptual contributions: eager table consolidation, in which a composite table is assembled early in the wrangling phase via interactive, incremental assembly of multiple source tables; and a declarative vocabulary for table consolidation consisting of two operations, Stack and Pack. We evaluate the system through a replication study in which the authors reproduce 17 published journalist programming workflows using only the interface, and a deployment study with four professional data journalists. The replication study demonstrates expressive coverage of real-world consolidation tasks. The deployment study confirms utility for practitioners who understand joins conceptually but lack the programming skills to execute them, and surfaces an unanticipated secondary value for data journalism education.

</details>


### [177] [A Multiplexing Design Space: Theory, Method, and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12719)
*Yiwen Xing,Afrah Farea,Saiful Khan,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在约束应用场景下探索视觉复用设计空间的方法，并以机器学习工作流中多个二维标量场的可视化为例，通过三步设计流程和领域专家参与，确定了最优的默认复用设计方案及用户可控制的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉复用现象在可视化设计中普遍存在但鲜少被系统性地探索，设计空间庞大，需要一种约束条件下的设计方法来指导实践。

Method: 提出三步设计方法，包括预设计阶段的领域基础与理论分析，以及在多二维标量场场景下系统探索视觉复用设计空间，结合领域专家的协同设计与评估。

Result: 识别出相对最优的默认复用设计方案，以及领域专家可通过用户界面控制的小变体需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的约束性设计方法能有效缩小视觉复用设计空间，并通过领域专家参与产生实用的设计方案，为类似场景提供可推广的设计框架。

Abstract: Many visualization designs feature phenomena referred to as ``visual multiplexing'', where multiple pieces of information associated with the same data point are conveyed simultaneously. Although visualization designers are able to bring such phenomena, often unconsciously, into their designs, the design space of visual multiplexing is huge, and it is uncommon to explore visual multiplexing systematically as design patterns. In this paper, we propose a design method for exploring a smaller design space constrained by an application. As an illustrative case study, we focus on machine learning (ML) workflows for developing ML models that approximate partial differential equations (PDEs). In these workflows, ML researchers need to analyze the inter-relationships among multiple 2D scalar fields frequently. Since superimposing one heatmap on top of another is not an effective design, we formulate three design steps to explore the design space of visual multiplexing in the context of multiple 2D scalar fields. Our design method also includes a pre-design step for domain grounding and theoretical analysis, and involves domain experts in both co-design and evaluation activities. The design process enables us to identify relatively optimal default multiplexing designs as well as the need for small variations that domain experts can control through a user interface.

</details>


### [178] [Exploring How Agent Voice Accents Shape Human-AI Collaboration in K-12 Group Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12805)
*Prerna Ravi,Carúmey Stevens,Ben Hurt,Brandon Hanks,Grace Lin,Emma Anderson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过33名教师的混合方法研究，发现GenAI语音代理的口音（英式、印度式、非裔美国人式）显著影响小组协作中的心智模型、角色分配、信任和参与度；英式口音代理被视为工具，而印度式和非裔美国人式口音代理更易被拟人化并作为同伴融入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作是21世纪教育的核心，但教师在促进学生互动方面持续面临挑战。LLM对话同伴代理为调节面对面小组工作提供了新可能，然而代理的人设设计（尤其是声音特征）如何影响学习者的感知、信任和互动动态尚不明确。

Method: 采用被试间混合方法，33名教师参与实验，考察不同口音（英式、印度式、非裔美国人式）的GenAI语音代理对协作和代理感知的影响；通过问卷、小组互动分析和人工制品进行数据收集。

Result: 口音塑造了参与者的心智模型和代理在小组互动中的角色：英式口音代理被当作工具，以疏离、实用性的方式参与；印度式和非裔美国人式口音代理则更易被拟人化并作为同伴融入。这些角色期望影响了信任、参与度和依赖程度。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了对GenAI社会语言设计特征如何塑造CSCL中小组动力的理解，为设计面向小组学习的文化包容性AI伙伴提供了启示。

Abstract: Collaboration is widely recognized as a cornerstone of 21st-century education, yet teachers still encounter persistent challenges in fostering productive peer interaction. LLM conversational peer agents introduce new possibilities for mediating in-person group work, raising questions about how persona design, particularly their voice characteristics, shapes learners' perceptions, trust, and interactional dynamics. While prior work has examined agent accent effects in one-to-one settings, little is known about how these effects manifest in groups. We conducted a between-subjects mixed-methods study with 33 teachers examining how a GenAI voice agent with different accents (British, Indian, and African American) influenced collaboration and agent perception. Across surveys, group interaction analyses, and artifacts, we find that accent shaped participants' mental models and the roles the agent assumed in group interaction. The British-accented agent was largely treated as a tool and engaged in detached, utility-based ways, whereas Indian- and African American-accented agents were more readily anthropomorphized and integrated as peers. These role expectations influenced trust, engagement, and reliance over time. This work advances understanding of how GenAI's sociolinguistic design features shape group dynamics in CSCL, with implications for designing culturally inclusive AI partners in group learning.

</details>


### [179] [From Prompts to Preferences: An Open-Source Platform for Generative AI-Enhanced Conjoint Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12972)
*Philipp Brauner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源、自托管的联合分析调查平台，集成了生成式AI（LLM和文生图模型）来创建文本和视觉刺激，并提供了设置向导、AI辅助属性建议和实时数据分析功能，通过智能家居护理机器人偏好研究（N=55）验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联合分析工具要么昂贵，要么缺乏端到端调查基础设施，使得没有商业平台访问权限的研究者面临重大障碍。

Method: 开发了一个开源自托管Web应用，核心创新是利用生成式AI（LLM生成文本场景描述，文生图模型生成视觉刺激）将联合分析配置文件参数化到研究者定义的基础提示中，同时提供结构化设置向导、AI辅助属性建议和实时数据分析等降低技术门槛的功能。

Result: 通过一项关于环境辅助生活（AAL）中护理机器人偏好的概念验证研究（N=55），使用AI生成的视觉刺激验证了平台的可行性。

Conclusion: AI辅助可以拓宽HCI及相关领域的方法论储备，但理论根基必须仍然是研究者的责任。该平台通过完整的导出包（包含所有刺激、生成提示和响应数据）促进了透明度和可重复性。

Abstract: Conjoint analysis is a widely used preference measurement method in marketing research, political science, healthcare, and human-computer interaction. Despite broad adoption, researchers without access to commercial platforms face significant barriers, as existing tools are either expensive or lack end-to-end survey infrastructure. This paper presents an open-source, self-hosted web application for designing, deploying, and analysing conjoint surveys. Beyond conventional tabular stimuli, the platform uses generative AI to produce integrated stimuli formats: textual scenario descriptions generated by a large language model, and visual stimuli by a text-to-image model. A researcher-defined base prompt is parameterised with the conjoint profile, and optional LLM-facing level annotations enrich the generation. A structured setup wizard, AI-assisted attribute suggestion, and live data analysis lower the technical barriers for researchers new to conjoint methodology. A full export bundle including all stimuli, their generating prompts, and response data facilitates transparency and reproducibility. The platform is demonstrated through a proof-of-concept study on care robot preferences for ambient assisted living (AAL, N=55) using AI-generated visual stimuli. The paper discusses the role of AI assistance in conjoint design, arguing that theoretical grounding must remain the researcher's responsibility, and outlining how genAI-generated stimuli can broaden the methodological repertoire for HCI and related fields.

</details>


### [180] [Mod-Guide: An LLM-based Content Moderation Feedback System to Address Insensitive Speech toward Indigenous Ethnic and Religious Minority Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13397)
*Dipto Das,Achhiya Sultana,Ankit Singh Chauhan,Saadia Binte Alam,Mohammad Shidujaman,Shion Guha,Sunandan Chakraborty,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM内容审核系统对孟加拉国印度教和查克马少数族群的文化不敏感言论的识别局限，并通过检索增强生成（RAG）技术融入社群叙事，开发了Mod-Guide工具，显著提升了模型对少数群体视角的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在内容审核中可能无法识别针对少数族群的隐性文化不敏感言论（如忽视、歪曲或规范性框架），而现有研究多关注显性仇恨言论。论文旨在揭示LLM的认知局限，并探索将少数群体视角纳入审核系统的可行方法。

Method: 与孟加拉国印度教和查克马社群成员共同创建文化敏感言论语料库，将社群叙事通过检索增强生成（RAG）技术整合至审核流程，开发Mod-Guide工具；通过混合方法评估（少数族群与多数族群参与者）验证效果。

Result: RAG增强的审核回应在语境准确性上显著提升，且不同族群（少数与多数）对该回应的感知存在差异。

Conclusion: 本研究通过强调修复正义和诠释学包容，推动了人机交互、AI伦理和社会计算领域的发展，为设计更具文化敏感性的内容审核系统提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Language operates as a mechanism of both marginalization and resistance, especially for minority communities navigating insensitive and harmful speech online. As content moderation increasingly depends on large language models (LLMs), concerns arise about whether these systems can recognize culturally insensitive speech-language that disregards or marginalizes the cultural and religious perspectives of historically underrepresented communities, often through implicit erasure, misrepresentation, or normative framing, rather than overt hostility. Focusing on Bangladesh's Hindu and Chakma communities -- the country's largest religious and Indigenous ethnic minorities, respectively -- this paper investigates the epistemic limits of LLM-based moderation systems and explores methods for incorporating minority perspectives. We co-created a culturally grounded corpus of insensitive speech with community members and integrated their narratives into moderation pipelines using retrieval augmented generation (RAG). Our tool, Mod-Guide, improves LLM sensitivity to minority viewpoints by leveraging contextual cues derived from lived experience. Through mixed-method evaluations involving both minority and majority participants, we demonstrate that RAG-enhanced moderation responses are more contextually accurate and perceived differently across ethnic lines. This work advances research in human-computer interaction, AI ethics, and social computing by foregrounding restorative justice and hermeneutical inclusion in the design of content moderation systems.

</details>


### [181] [Ride, Track, and Recover: Pilot Randomized Trial of a Wearable Digital Self-Management Intervention During a Veteran Endurance-Cycling Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13529)
*Alan Ta,Nilsu Salgin,Caleb Armstrong,Kala Phillips Reindel,Farzan Sasangohar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究在一项自然主义设定的骑行活动中，将13名PTSD退伍军人随机分为数字干预+身体活动组与仅身体活动组，并增设7名居家监测对照。通过智能手表连续监测高唤醒事件，结合每周自评量表，发现数字干预组的基线归一化高唤醒轨迹表现出结构化稳定，而仅身体活动组在后期出现症状升级；数字干预组在耐力活动中症状改善更持续。ML检测感知精度与症状严重程度正相关。结果表明可穿戴检测结合数字自我管理工具可能支持高唤醒稳定化与症状改善，同时强调个性化与以人为中心的设计在可穿戴心理健康系统中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退伍军人PTSD的高唤醒、焦虑和抑郁症状在临床环境外难以监测和管理，现有方法依赖临床就诊，缺乏生态效度和实时性。本研究旨在探索通过可穿戴设备与数字干预结合的方式，在自然主义环境中实现症状的连续监测与自我管理。

Method: 13名参与Project Hero骑行活动的退伍军人通过计算机生成序列随机分配至数字干预+身体活动组（n=7）或仅身体活动组（n=3），另从Project Hero社区选取7名居家监测对照（最终分析n=4）。使用智能手表连续采集心率和加速度特征检测高唤醒事件，参与者实时确认。每周收集焦虑、抑郁和PTSD严重程度的自评量表数据。采用广义加性混合模型（GAMM）刻画非线性轨迹。

Result: 基线归一化高唤醒轨迹在三种条件下存在显著差异：数字干预组表现出结构化稳定化，仅身体活动组在后期出现症状升级。两组骑行组在耐力活动中均出现急性症状改善，但数字干预组的获益维持程度更高。居家对照组呈现渐进性症状下降。ML检测感知精度在不同个体间差异显著，与症状严重程度呈正相关，高症状参与者确认了更大比例的检测事件。

Conclusion: 将可穿戴检测与数字自我管理工具相结合，可能有助于支持PTSD高唤醒的稳定化和症状改善，同时强调了在可穿戴心理健康系统中个性化与以人为中心设计的重要性。

Abstract: Post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD) in veterans is characterized by persistent hyperarousal and comorbid anxiety and depressive symptoms that are difficult to monitor and manage outside clinical settings. Thirteen veterans participating in a Project Hero cycling event in Texas were randomized by computer-generated sequence in a naturalistic setting to two arms: (1) digital intervention plus physical activity, or (2) physical activity only, plus a third at-home monitoring control cohort consisting of 7 veterans selected from the broader Project Hero veteran community. Continuous smartwatch sensing combined heart rate and accelerometer features to detect hyperarousal events, which were confirmed in real time by participants. Weekly self-report measures of anxiety, depression, and PTSD severity were collected. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear trajectories over time. Baseline-normalized hyperarousal trajectories differed significantly across conditions, with the digital intervention group (n=7) showing structured stabilization compared to late-study escalation in the physical-only group (n=3). Both cycling groups exhibited acute symptom improvements during the endurance event; however, the digital intervention group demonstrated a higher overall maintenance of gains. The at-home control group (n=4) showed gradual symptom declines. Perceived precision of ML detections varied substantially across individuals and was positively associated with symptom severity, with higher-severity participants confirming a greater proportion of detected events. These results suggest that coupling wearable detection with digital self-management tools may support stabilization of hyperarousal and symptom improvement while emphasizing the importance of personalization and human-centered design in wearable mental health systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Characterizing Tests in IoT Software: Practices, Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12592)
*Rufeng Chen,Hengcheng Zhu,Wuqi Zhang,Zixu Zhou,Lili Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文首次对开源物联网软件的测试用例进行了实证研究，发现尽管物联网软件包含大量测试，但其有效性有限，主要挑战在于管理复杂的外部依赖交互，并指出mock对象的使用与这些挑战高度契合，有望提升测试覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网软件中的缺陷可能导致严重后果（如智能锁被非法入侵），但目前对物联网软件如何被测试知之甚少，因此需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 对开源物联网软件的测试用例进行了首次实证研究，评估了测试用例的有效性，探究了测试中的挑战，并分析了mock对象的使用情况。

Result: 物联网软件虽包含大量测试，但有效性有限；主要测试挑战是管理与多种外部依赖（网络组件、文件系统、操作系统、数据库等）的复杂交互；mock对象的使用与这些挑战高度一致。

Conclusion: mock对象的使用与物联网软件测试中的主要挑战高度契合，表明mocking是增强测试覆盖率、应对物联网软件测试复杂性的潜在解决方案。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) is experiencing rapid growth. Smart devices are emerging in smart homes and industrial applications, performing mission-critical tasks. Bugs in IoT software can lead to severe consequences. For example, a buggy smart lock can allow unauthorized access to a private property. Testing is a primary practice to expose software bugs and ensure software quality. However, little is known about how IoT software is tested. To bridge this gap, we conducted the first empirical study on test cases in open-source IoT software. Specifically, we evaluated the effectiveness of test cases in IoT software, explored the challenges inherent in testing IoT software, and analyzed the usage of mock objects. Our results indicate that while IoT software often contains a considerable number of tests, their effectiveness remains limited. We identified the primary challenges in testing IoT software as managing complex interactions with various external dependencies, such as other network-reliant IoT components, file systems, operating systems, and databases. We also observed that the use of mock objects in IoT software closely aligns with our identified testing challenges. This alignment demonstrates the potential of mocking as a solution to enhance test coverage and address the complexities of IoT software testing.

</details>


### [183] [HybridCodeAuthorship: A Benchmark Dataset for Line-Level Code Authorship Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12620)
*Luke Patterson,Li Wang,Adam Faulkner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了HybridCodeAuthorship基准数据集，用于检测AI与人类混合编写的代码。该数据集基于CodeSearchNet构建，包含交错的人机代码行，模拟真实AI辅助编程场景。实验显示最优算法AIGCode Detector在块级和行级检测上F1分数仅为0.48和0.56，表明该任务具有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成代码检测基准多基于学术性LeetCode风格问题，且假设代码片段完全由人或AI编写，无法反映真实工业代码库中AI辅助编程产生的混合代码场景，因此需要更真实的基准数据集。

Method: 利用CodeSearchNet中的GitHub开源仓库链接构建数据集，生成包含人类与AI交错编写的Python代码文件；使用两种SOTA检测算法在行级和块级进行性能评估。

Result: 最佳算法AIGCode Detector在块级检测F1得分为0.48，行级检测F1得分为0.56，表现有限，说明该基准具有挑战性。

Conclusion: HybridCodeAuthorship填补了真实AI辅助编程场景下混合代码检测基准的空白，现有算法在该任务上性能不足，亟需更优的检测方法。

Abstract: Thanks to the rapid adoption of AI code assistants powered by large language models (LLMs), industry codebases are, increasingly, a hybrid of AI- and human-authored code. For risk management and productivity analysis purposes, it is crucial to enable fine-grained location detection of AI-generated code. To develop algorithms for this task, quality benchmarks are needed to assess performance. However, existing benchmarks tend to comprise academic, LeetCode-style problems and presume a code snippet is either completely human-authored or completely AI-authored, which is not reflective of the diverse intents and styles of industry codebases utilizing AI code assistants. To fill these gaps, we introduce HybridCodeAuthorship, a novel benchmark of Python code files with interleaved human- and AI-authored lines of code to simulate authentic utilization of AI code assistants. In this paper, we first present our dataset construction pipeline, which leverages CodeSearchNet, a massive collection of links to open sourced repositories on GitHub. We then benchmark the performance of two state-of-the-art AI-generated code detection algorithms at both the line- and chunk-level. Experimental results demonstrate that HybridCodeAuthorship is a challenging benchmark with a top-scoring algorithm, AIGCode Detector, obtaining a highest F1 score of 0.48 and 0.56 on chunk-level and line-level code detection tasks, respectively.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Problem Solving: UOJ-Bench for Evaluating Code Generation, Hacking, and Repair in Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12864)
*Tingqiang Xu,Hangrui Zhou,Tianle Cai,Alex Gu,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了UOJ-Bench基准，评估LLM在编程教育场景中的代码生成、代码攻击和代码修复能力。结果表明，即使最强模型在一次性评估中识别错误率低于50%，测试时扩展虽能将成功率提升至90%以上但计算成本高昂。前沿LLM能发现约5%满分提交中的错误，为在线评测系统提供补充信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在竞赛编程中表现强劲，但其在支持人类学习方面的作用尚未被充分探索，尤其是识别人类代码错误这一关键教学能力。

Method: 构建UOJ-Bench基准，包含代码生成、代码攻击（识别错误）和代码修复三个任务；数据来自UOJ真实代码提交，通过UOJ原生评测系统评估。

Result: 一次性评估下最强模型识别错误率低于50%；测试时扩展可将成功率提升至90%以上但计算成本高；前沿模型能发现约5%满分提交中的错误。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在代码错误识别方面能力有限，测试时扩展虽有效但不实用；前沿模型已能为在线评测系统提供互补性错误发现信号。

Abstract: Despite strong performance in competitive programming, the role of Large Language Models (LLMs) in supporting human learning in the same setting remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce UOJ-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate not only the problem-solving ability of LLMs, but also their ability to identify errors in human-written code -- a crucial educational activity traditionally supported by running test cases over online judge systems. UOJ-Bench consists of three distinct tasks: code generation, code hacking, and code repair, all constructed from real-world code submissions on the Universal Online Judge (UOJ) and evaluated through UOJ's native judging infrastructure. Our results show that under one-shot evaluation, even the strongest models fail to identify errors in more than 50% of a set of submissions that have been found to be incorrect by UOJ users. While test-time scaling improves success rates to above 90%, the substantial computational costs incurred from model inference limit its practicality for large-scale deployment. Despite these limitations, we find that the best-performing models under test-time scaling can uncover errors in over 5% of full-score submissions across roughly 30 problems, suggesting that frontier LLMs can already provide complementary signals beyond standard judging systems.

</details>


### [185] [The Rise of AI-Native Software Engineering: Implications for Practice, Education, and the Future Workforce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12986)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述48篇经同行评审的高影响力文献（2016-2026），建立了AI原生软件工程的概念框架，涵盖意图、协作与验证三大支柱，提出了九维度能力模型、四阶段大学课程路线图、教师发展与劳动力转型策略，以及十一项研究空白优先级议程。研究发现LLM-for-SE产出自2022年末以来增长约五倍，但生产力效应证据存在内在矛盾，强调工程师的判断力、验证与编排能力比代码生产更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI、LLM及新兴的Agentic AI正在深刻改变软件工程的开发流程、所需能力、职业角色及大学教育目标，亟需对现有研究证据进行系统性整合，以指导实践、教育与劳动力转型。

Method: 采用四智能体研究工作流（文献发现、科学计量分析、课程转型、劳动力影响），从软件工程、机器学习、计算教育、人机协作和软件生产力等顶会顶刊中检索、筛选并分析了48篇经核实的高影响力同行评审文献（2016-2026）。

Result: 发现LLM-for-SE领域年度产出自2022年末后增长约五倍；综合出九大主题和三条轨迹（实践、教育、劳动力）的证据；提出AI原生软件工程概念框架（意图、协作、验证）、九维度能力模型、四阶段大学课程路线图及十一项研究空白。

Conclusion: 生产力效应的证据存在内在矛盾，收益高度依赖具体情境；AI原生时代软件工程教育的核心挑战在于培养工程师的判断力、验证与编排能力，而非仅关注代码生产。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI), Large Language Models (LLMs), and emerging Agentic AI constitute the most disruptive transformation in the history of software engineering (SE), reshaping development processes, required competencies, professional roles, and the educational outcomes that universities must deliver. This paper presents a systematic review of 48 verified, influential peer-reviewed publications (2016--2026) drawn from leading venues in software engineering, machine learning, computing education, human--AI collaboration, and software productivity. Studies were discovered, screened, and analyzed through a four-agent research workflow (Literature Discovery, Scientometric Analysis, Curriculum Transformation, and Workforce Impact) and were verified against primary sources. We synthesize the evidence along nine themes and three trajectories -- practice, education, and workforce -- and report a scientometric inflection in which annual LLM-for-SE output grew roughly five-fold after late 2022. From this synthesis we contribute: (i) a conceptual framework for AI-native software engineering organized around \emph{intent}, \emph{collaboration}, and \emph{verification}; (ii) a nine-dimension competency model spanning specification, critical evaluation, agent orchestration, and metacognition; (iii) a four-phase university curriculum roadmap with AI-resilient assessment; (iv) faculty-development and workforce-transformation strategies; and (v) a prioritized agenda of eleven research gaps. The evidence base is internally contradictory on the magnitude and direction of productivity effects, underscoring that benefits are strongly context-dependent and that educating engineers for judgment, verification, and orchestration -- rather than code production alone -- is the central challenge of the AI-native era.

</details>


### [186] [The End of Code Review: Coding Agents Supersede Human Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13175)
*Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文论证了基于LLM的编码代理已具备足够能力，使得传统人工代码审查不再是软件质量管线的必要组成部分；编码代理能够以更低成本和更高吞吐量实现代码审查的所有既定目标，而“代理写代码+人工审查”的简单整合模式既无法提供有意义的保证，也无法跟上AI辅助的开发速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战自1976年Fagan正式化代码审查以来持续近五十年的行业基石实践，论证在编码代理能力跨越临界点的背景下，传统人工代码审查的必要性已不复存在。

Method: 基于两个核心论据进行论证：(1) 代码审查的所有既定目标都能由编码代理以更低成本和更高吞吐量实现；(2) “代理编写代码+人类强制审查”的简单整合模式是死胡同，既无法提供有意义的保证，也无法随AI辅助吞吐量扩展。

Result: 提出编码代理已跨越能力阈值，传统人工代码审查不再是软件质量管线的必要组成部分这一核心结论。

Conclusion: 应重新审视传统代码审查的必要性，转向更高效的基于编码代理的质量保障范式。

Abstract: Code review has been the primary quality gate in software development since Fagan formalised code inspection in 1976. For five decades, having a human examine and comment on a colleague's changes before merge has been a cornerstone practice at organisations of every size. Coding agents are large language model (LLM)-based autonomous systems capable of reading, writing, testing, and repairing software. We argue that coding agents have crossed a threshold of capability at which traditional human code review is no longer a necessary component of a software quality pipeline. Our argument rests on two claims: every stated goal of code review can be served by agents at lower cost and higher throughput; the naive integration in which agents write code and humans remain the mandatory reviewers is a dead end because it neither provides meaningful assurance nor scales with AI-assisted throughput.

</details>


### [187] [ComAct: Reframing Professional Software Manipulation via COM-as-Action Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13239)
*Jiaxin Ai,Tao Hu,Xuemeng Yang,Shu Zou,Hairong Zhang,Daocheng Fu,Yu Yang,Hongbin Zhou,Nianchen Deng,Pinlong Cai,Zhongyuan Wang,Botian Shi,Kaipeng Zhang,Licheng Wen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出COM-as-Action范式，将专业软件交互重构为确定性程序合成而非视觉控制，并构建工业CAD基准ComCADBench。实验表明基于COM的执行远超GUI方法，开发的ComActor模型通过三阶段渐进训练实现SOTA性能，在长程任务中表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用代理在专业软件操作中存在根本局限：基于GUI的代理受困于脆弱的视觉定位和长程误差累积，而基于API的方法难以处理异构协议和不可访问的商业接口。本文旨在寻找一种统一的可执行抽象来解决这些问题。

Method: (1) 识别Component Object Model (COM)作为统一可执行抽象，提出COM-as-Action范式，将专业软件交互重构为确定性程序合成；(2) 构建ComCADBench基准，用于评估工业CAD软件中的代理性能；(3) 开发ComActor自纠错代理，采用渐进式三阶段训练框架；(4) 构建ComForge可扩展平台，支持Windows容器中的大规模训练。

Result: 前沿商业模型在GUI交互下近乎零成功率，而基于COM的执行带来显著即时收益；ComActor在ComCADBench上达到SOTA性能，在基线模型崩溃的长程任务中展现出强鲁棒性，并能泛化到外部CAD基准。

Conclusion: COM-as-Action范式为专业软件操作提供了根本性突破，通过程序合成替代视觉控制有效解决了GUI方法的固有限制，证明了基于COM的确定性执行在工业级软件自动化中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Existing computer-use agents remain fundamentally limited in professional software manipulation: GUI-based agents suffer from fragile visual grounding and long-horizon error accumulation, while API-basedapproaches struggle with heterogeneous protocols and inaccessible commercial interfaces. In this work,we identify the Component Object Model (COM) as a unified executable abstraction, proposing COM-as-Action: a new paradigm that reframes professional software interaction as deterministic program synthesisrather than sequential visual control. To validate this paradigm in the most demanding environments, weintroduce ComCADBench, the first benchmark for agents operating real industrial CAD software. Ourexperiments reveal a substantial paradigm gap: frontier proprietary models achieve near-zero successunder GUI-based interaction, whereas COM-based execution yields substantial immediate gains. Tobridge the remaining gap between syntactic correctness and geometric accuracy, we develop ComActor, aself-correcting agent trained through a progressive three-stage framework, alongside ComForge, a scalableplatform for large-scale training in Windows containers. Extensive experiments show that ComActorachieves state-of-the-art performance on ComCADBench, with strong resilience in long-horizon taskswhere baselines collapse, and generalizes to external CAD benchmark.

</details>


### [188] [Mining Architectural Quality Under Agentic AI Adoption: A Causal Study of Java Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13298)
*Oliver Aleksander Larsen,Mahyar T. Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对151个开源Java仓库（其中74个检测到AI代理工具采用）进行1,811个月度Arcan快照分析，采用交错双重差分法，发现AI编码工具使代码量增长12.8%（p=0.003），但架构坏味总数基本不变（+1.1%, p=0.82），导致架构坏味密度下降6.7%（p=0.004），这本质上是分母效应而非架构质量改善。研究警告：密度归一化指标在处理方法影响系统规模时会产生误导性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果研究仅测量了代码层面的影响（复杂度、静态分析警告），但AI编码工具对软件架构层面的影响尚属未知。本研究旨在填补这一空白，提供AI工具采用对架构质量因果效应的证据。

Method: 挖掘151个开源Java仓库（74个检测到AI代理采用，77个倾向得分匹配对照），每个仓库13个月窗口生成1,811个月度Arcan架构快照。采用交错双重差分设计（staggered DiD）和Borusyak插值估计量，应用野聚类引导程序（wild cluster bootstrap）、Lee边界（Lee bounds）和过时观测敏感性分析（stale-observation sensitivity）进行稳健性检验。

Result: AI工具采用后：代码总量增长12.8%（p=0.003）；架构坏味总数基本不变（+1.1%, p=0.82）；架构坏味密度下降6.7%（p=0.004）——但这是分母效应（代码量增长稀释了密度）而非架构质量实际改善。各坏味类型的分析及多项稳健性检验均支持这一模式，平行趋势假设成立（Wald p=0.90）。

Conclusion: AI编码工具确实被广泛采用，但并未改善或恶化软件架构质量（坏味总数不变）。密度指标的下降完全由代码量增长驱动。未来因果挖掘研究AI工具影响时，必须同时报告原始计数和明确分解，不能仅依赖密度归一化指标。研究提供的完整150+仓库月度面板数据已公开。

Abstract: AI coding tools are now used by a majority of developers, and agentic use of these tools has popularized the practice colloquially called "vibe coding". Yet causal evidence on their effect on software architecture is scarce. Prior causal work has measured code-level outcomes (complexity, static analysis warnings); whether such degradation propagates to architecture-level outcomes remains unknown. We mine 151 open-source Java repositories, 74 with detectable agentic AI adoption (identified via configuration files and Co-Authored-By commit trailers) and 77 propensity-matched controls, across a 13-month per-repository window yielding 1,811 monthly Arcan snapshots. We estimate the causal effect of adoption on architectural smell density (ASD) with a staggered difference-in-differences design and the Borusyak imputation estimator, applying a causal design recently used for code-level metrics to the architecture level. Total smell counts are essentially unchanged (+1.1%, p = 0.82) while lines of code grow +12.8% (p = 0.003); the resulting 6.7% ASD decline (p = 0.004) is therefore a denominator effect rather than an architectural improvement. Per-type estimates and robustness checks (wild cluster bootstrap, Lee bounds, stale-observation sensitivity) corroborate the pattern; pre-trends are flat (Wald p = 0.90), consistent with parallel trends. Density-normalized outcomes can mislead when treatment affects system size: raw counts and explicit decomposition are required for causal mining studies of AI tool adoption. The complete replication package, including the curated 151-repository monthly panel, is publicly available.

</details>


### [189] [Toward Instructions-as-Code: Understanding the Impact of Instruction Files on Agentic Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13449)
*Ali Arabat,Mohammed Sayagh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析15,549个AI代理生成的PR（来自148个项目），研究指令文件对AI代理性能的影响。结果发现，创建指令文件并不必然带来更好的结果：27.7%的项目合并率提升至少20%，而26.35%的项目合并率下降。成功提升合并率的项目通常拥有更长、结构更完善的指令文件。研究呼吁将指令文件开发视为一项软件工程活动（即Instructions-as-Code）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI代理指令文件的创建与代理性能（PR合并率、处理复杂任务能力、合并所需努力）之间的关系。

Method: 基于AIDev数据集中148个项目的15,549个Agentic PR，从合并率、代码变更量、合并努力三个维度，比较每个项目在创建指令文件前后的表现差异。

Result: 指令文件并不必然提升AI代理性能：27.7%的项目合并率提升≥20%，26.35%的项目下降；代码变更量和合并努力方面同样呈现混合结果；成功项目的指令文件更长且结构更完善。

Conclusion: 需要将指令文件开发作为软件工程活动（Instructions-as-Code）进行系统化研究，以帮助从业者更好指导AI代理。

Abstract: AI-agents (e.g., GitHub Copilot) collaborate as teammates in different software engineering tasks, including code generation proposed through pull requests (Agentic-PRs). For better agent efficiency, developers create instruction files that guide the AI-agents, including how to navigate the project, locate the right components, run tests, respect best practices, and more. In this paper, we investigate the relationship between the creation of these instructions and the performance of AI-agents in creating better pull requests, which have a higher chance of success (i.e., the merge rate), address more complex tasks (e.g., code churn), and require less effort to be merged (e.g., time to merge). To this end, we analyze 15,549 agentic PRs from 148 projects in the AIDev dataset. Using the three dimensions, we compare each project before and after the creation of the instruction files. We find that specifying instructions for AI-agents does not necessarily lead to better results. With the instruction files, 27.7\% of the projects increased their merge rate by at least 20\%, while 26.35\% decreased it. The same observation is seen with the amount of changes (e.g., code churn, number of modified files) and with the efforts to merge an agentic PR (e.g., merge time and number of comments). From a first exploration, we find that projects that managed to increase their merge rate have substantially longer instruction files, which are also well structured into a higher number of sections and sub-sections. Our results motivate the need for research to assist practitioners in framing the development of instruction files as a software engineering activity (aka, \textbf{Instructions-as-Code}).

</details>


### [190] [Understanding the Rejection of Fixes Generated by Agentic Pull Requests -- Insights from the AIDev Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13468)
*Mahmoud Abujadallah,Ali Arabat,Mohammed Sayagh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对AIDev数据集的探索，发现AI编码智能体（Copilot、Devin、Cursor、Claude）生成的PR中有46.41%被拒绝。作者对306个未合并的PR进行了定性和定量分析，归纳出14种拒绝原因，分为四类：实现不正确、CI流水线失败、无法执行实现、优先级低。研究建议在方法提示、约束限定和验证指导三个层面改进AI智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体生成的代码修复中有近一半被拒绝，造成大量人力资源和计算资源的浪费。理解AI智能体失败的模式对于更好地将其整合为高效团队伙伴至关重要。

Method: 对AIDev数据集中306个由AI智能体创建或合著但未被合并的PR样本进行定性研究，随后进行定量分析以确定拒绝原因的分类和分布。

Result: 识别出14种具体拒绝原因，归纳为四大类别：实现不正确（如不完整、错误方法）、CI流水线失败（测试未通过）、无法执行实现（如未生成代码、会话丢失）、优先级低。定量揭示了各类原因的比例分布。

Conclusion: AI智能体需要三个层面的改进：提供修复问题的思路提示、明确不应采取的方法约束、指导如何通过CI验证而不引入破坏性变更。同时需要良好的任务优先级排序，避免浪费人工审查和计算资源。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly used to generate pull requests (PRs) that propose code fixes in software projects. From a first exploration of the AIDev dataset, we find that 46.41\% of the fixes proposed by the agents Copilot, Devin, Cursor, and Claude are rejected. This represents a significant amount of wasted resources that require human reviews, verifications, and running tests and validations for fixes that are merely discarded. Our goal in this paper is to understand the failure modes of AI-agents, an understanding that is crucial for better integrating AI-agents as efficient teammates. In this paper, we conduct a qualitative study on a representative sample of 306 non-merged pull requests created or co-authored by the agents mentioned earlier, followed by a quantitative analysis of the reasons for rejection. Our qualitative findings identify 14 reasons divided into four high-level categories for rejecting AI-agent fixes. We observe that developers can reject fixes due to fixes whose implementation is incorrect (e.g., incomplete, wrong approach), fixes that do not pass the continuous integration (CI) pipelines and fail tests, fixes for which the agent is unable to perform the implementation (e.g., no code generated, sessions lost), and fixes whose priority is low. Our results shed light on the importance of better guiding the model at these levels: (1) proposing hints about the approach to follow for fixing an issue, (2) outlining constraints or limitations regarding the approaches that should not be taken, and (3) instructing the agent on how to validate the implementation through CI pipelines and without introducing a breaking change. Our results suggest the need for good prioritization of tasks so that generated fixes do not lead to wasted human review efforts or wasted agent resources (e.g., tokens, compute, or allowed number of requests).

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [A catalog of fast matrix multiplication algorithms with frontier-closure search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13408)
*Benoit Chatain Lacelle*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一、机器可验证的矩阵乘法算法目录，覆盖形状最高32×32×32的多种数域，并通过前沿闭包搜索方法重组目录条目，同时明确了“发现更优双线性核心”与“组合已知核心”这两条研究路径的非重叠性，解决了文献中的归属争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2022-2026年间小规模矩阵乘法领域涌现了大量分散的成果，它们分布在不同领域、遵循不同署名惯例和使用不同序列化格式，缺乏统一归类的目录。同时，Perminov的开源翻转图框架在大规模格式空间中发现许多新的低秩方案，进一步增加了整合的需求。

Method: 构建涵盖32×32×32以内形状、支持有理数、整数、实数、复数及F₂数域的机器可验证统一目录，并单独分类交换算法。采用前沿闭包搜索方法，通过轴翻转、Kronecker积、轴拼接、偶然乘积、带分配的重组（可选输出剥离和配对融合）以及向下投影等操作重组目录条目。

Result: 提出了一个统一、可自动更新的矩阵乘法算法目录，并刷新了DIS09对比表格，按数域和交换性分列。核心方法论贡献是阐明了重组操作与手工构建算法之间的非重叠性质，解决了文献中的归属困惑。

Conclusion: 该工作为矩阵乘法算法的系统性编目和比较提供了基础设施，明确了不同研究路径的边界，并提供了自动更新的工具链。

Abstract: The 2022--2026 burst of activity in small-format matrix multiplication (AlphaTensor 2022, AlphaEvolve 2025, Schwartz--Zwecher 2025) has produced striking individual results but scattered them across different fields, attribution conventions, and serialisation formats. A complementary line of work -- Perminov's open-source flip-graph framework~\cite{perminov2026fast,perminov2025fast} -- instead drives existing construction methods, notably flip-graph and \emph{meta-flip-graph} search, at scale across large format spaces, discovering many new low-rank schemes (including ternary-integer ones) that further enrich the landscape this catalog must unify. We present a unified, machine-checkable catalog covering shapes up to \nmpshape{32}{32}{32} over \Rationals, \Integers, \Reals, \Complex, and \Ftwo, with a separate axis for commutative algorithms (Waksman 1970, Makarov 1986, Rosowski 2019).
  Derivation over this catalog is performed by a \emph{frontier-closure search} that recombines catalog entries by axis-flip, Kronecker, axis concatenation, serendipitous products, recombination-with-allocation (with optional output peeling and pair fusion), and downward projection.
  A central methodological point is the \emph{non-overlap property}: our recombination does not, and cannot, rediscover the shared bilinear products that hand-crafted constructions (Strassen, Laderman, Smirnov, AlphaTensor) are built around. This draws a clean line between the ``find a cleverer bilinear core'' and ``compose known cores'' axes of progress, and resolves several attribution puzzles in the literature.
  We refresh the DIS09 comparison tables, split per field and with a commutative column, and provide the tooling to regenerate them automatically as the catalog evolves.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Influence Factors on RAG Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12469)
*Pedro Pereira,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça,Adrien Bécue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过432种配置的全因子实验，系统研究了检索增强生成（RAG）系统在面对投毒攻击时的鲁棒性，发现检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的关键因素，而生成器选择和数据库组成则对攻击成功率影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖外部检索内容进行推理，这种依赖引入了投毒攻击的脆弱性——敌对文档可操纵检索过程和生成输出。目前缺乏对RAG系统中投毒鲁棒性的系统性研究。

Method: 采用全因子实验设计，覆盖432种配置，系统分析数据集、检索器类型、检索深度、数据库组成、分块策略和生成器模型六个因素对检索级和生成级指标的影响。

Result: 检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的最强因素；生成器选择和数据库组成对下游攻击成功率影响显著；稠密检索和图检索相比BM25更具鲁棒性；更大的检索深度增加检索到投毒文档的概率；复制投毒内容到多个数据库会放大对抗性影响，而增加干净源可缓解该影响。

Conclusion: RAG系统的投毒脆弱性并非由单一组件导致，而是检索、生成和知识库配置三者相互作用的结果。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems enhance large language models by grounding responses in retrieved documents from external knowledge sources at inference time. However, this reliance on retrieved content introduces vulnerabilities to poisoning attacks, in which adversarial documents can manipulate both the retrieval process and the generated outputs. This paper investigates poisoning robustness in RAG through a full factorial experimental study covering 432 configurations. We analyze the impacts of dataset, retriever type, retrieval depth, database composition, chunking strategy, and generator model on retrieval-level and generation-level metrics. The results show that retriever architecture, dataset, and retrieval depth are the strongest factors affecting poisoning exposure, while generator choice and database composition have a major impact on downstream attack success. Dense and graph-based retrievers generally improve robustness relative to BM25, whereas larger retrieval depth increases the likelihood of retrieving poisoned passages. We further show that replicating poisoned content across multiple databases amplifies adversarial influence, while additional clean sources can mitigate it. These findings highlight that poisoning vulnerability in RAG is not attributable to a single component, but instead arises from the interaction of retrieval, generation, and knowledge-base configuration.

</details>


### [193] [From Parameters to Feature Space: Task Arithmetic for Backdoor Mitigation in Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12498)
*Zhenqian Zhu,Yamin Hu,Yiya Diao,Weixiang Li,Haodong Li,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出线性特征路径最小化（LFPM）框架，通过从特征空间视角引入反后门任务向量，在不损害干净任务性能的前提下有效缓解模型合并中的后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并虽成本低但易受后门攻击，现有基于任务算术的防御方法直接在参数空间编辑，难以在消除后门的同时保持干净任务性能。

Method: 在跨任务线性（CTL）框架下，从统一特征空间视角将反后门任务向量引入被后门攻击的合并模型，并采用基于梯度累积和损失路径积分的优化机制，沿插值路径实现稳健的后门抑制。

Result: 在全微调和参数高效微调（PEFT）设置下，LFPM对后门攻击均表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LFPM通过特征空间视角和路径积分优化，有效解决了模型合并中后门防御与干净性能保持的权衡问题。

Abstract: Model merging (MM) has gained significant attention as a cost-effective approach to integrate multiple task-specific models into a unified model. However, recent work reveals that MM is highly susceptible to backdoor attacks. Existing defenses based on task arithmetic often fail to eliminate backdoors without substantially degrading clean-task performance, owing to their reliance on direct parameter-space editing. To address this gap, we propose Linear Feature Path Minimization (LFPM), a backdoor mitigation framework for model merging, which introduces an anti-backdoor task vector into the backdoored merged model. Unlike prior approaches, LFPM formulates the backdoor robustness of the merged model from a unified feature-space perspective under the Cross-Task Linearity (CTL) framework, which leverages the approximate linearity of features across tasks. This perspective guides the optimization of the anti-backdoor task to suppress backdoors while preserving clean-task performance. Furthermore, we introduce an effective optimization mechanism based on gradient accumulation and loss path-integral, ensuring robust backdoor suppression along the interpolation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LFPM consistently exhibits strong robustness against backdoor attacks in both full fine-tuning and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) settings.

</details>


### [194] [Beyond Attack Success Rate: Examining Trigger Leakage in Vision-Language Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12586)
*Jiamin Chang,Salil Kanhere,Piotr Koniusz,Jason,Xue,Hammond Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉后门攻击中的"触发器泄漏"问题：与触发器视觉或语义相似的输入会无意中激活恶意行为。作者提出邻域泄漏率(NLR)来量化该问题，并通过实验表明标准微调会学习宽激活区域，而加入硬负样本可以缩小激活区域、减少泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击评估仅关注干净准确率和攻击成功率(ASR)，但未衡量攻击的"精确性"——即是否仅在预期触发条件下才激活隐藏行为。

Method: 形式化定义"触发器泄漏"问题，提出邻域泄漏率(NLR)指标；通过图标和文本触发器的近邻变体实验量化泄漏；使用文本触发器作为受控探针分析标准微调的学习行为；引入编辑距离为1的硬负样本训练策略来缩窄激活区域。

Result: 在3%投毒率下，图标和文本触发器的近邻变体泄漏严重：NLR分别达0.996和0.944；标准微调学习到的是宽激活区域而非精确触发条件；添加硬负样本可显著降低泄漏，在图像编辑和具身操作工作流中亦有效。

Conclusion: 视觉后门攻击的精确性至关重要，标准训练范式会导致触发器泄漏，而通过硬负样本训练可以提升触发条件的精确性，防止泄漏传播到可执行程序和动作序列中。

Abstract: Vision-Language Agentic Systems (VLAS) connect visual perception to planning, tool use, and physical actions. This means backdoor-type triggers can propagate through both decision pipelines and their connected interfaces, thus making visual backdoors a system-level threat. Current evaluations on such backdoors focus on clean accuracy and attack success rate (ASR), metrics that capture whether a trigger works, but not whether an attack is actually "precise" -- i.e. whether it triggers hidden behaviors only when intended. In this work, we formalize the failure of trigger precision as "trigger leakage": inputs that are visually or semantically close to the intended trigger and therefore inadvertently activate the attacker-specified behavior. To quantify this leakage, we introduce Neighbor Leakage Rate (NLR). Our experiments show that at a 3% poisoning ratio, icon and text triggers remain robust to common visual transformations, but their neighboring variants leak heavily, with NLR reaching 0.996 (icon) and 0.944 (text). Using textual triggers as a controlled probe, we show that standard fine-tuning learns a broad activation region rather than an exact trigger condition, causing neighboring strings to invoke the malicious behavior even when the exact trigger is absent. Adding edit-distance-one hard-negative samples during training substantially narrows this activation region and reduces leakage, including in image-editing and embodied-manipulation workflows, where leaked triggers can propagate into executable programs and action sequences.

</details>


### [195] [Amnesia: A Stealthy Replay Attack on Continual Learning Dreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12655)
*Ahmed Sharshar,Naveen Kumar Kummari,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Amnesia攻击，通过仅控制回放索引选择（而不修改像素、标签或模型参数），在满足可审计约束（类别分布偏差和回放率）的条件下，有效降低持续学习模型的准确率和反向迁移能力，揭示了索引级回放控制是一种实用且可审计的威胁面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习攻击多通过修改输入或训练流程（如投毒/后门）实现，缺乏明确的可审计约束，与真实场景脱节。本文旨在探索一种更现实的威胁：仅控制回放索引选择的受限权限内部攻击者，在满足可审计性约束下最大化模型性能退化。

Method: 提出Amnesia攻击方法，包含两步：(i) 计算轻量级类别效用（如EMA损失或置信度），将名义类别直方图p0向有害类别倾斜；(ii) 使用KL（指数倾斜）或TV（平衡质量重分配）优化器将倾斜投影回delta-ball内。窗口调度器执行滚动审计，满足可见性预算delta（TV/KL散度约束）和质量预算f（固定回放率）。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准和强回放基线中，Amnesia持续降低最终准确率并恶化反向迁移（-BWT）。KL变体在多种审计方案（逐批次和滚动窗口）下保持高度隐蔽性，TV变体破坏力更强但较易被检测。

Conclusion: 索引级回放控制是一种实用且可审计的持续学习威胁面，建立了原则性的影响-可见性权衡框架。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) models often use experience replay to reduce catastrophic forgetting, but their robustness to replay sampling interference remains underexplored. Existing CL attacks alter inputs or training pipelines (poisoning/backdoors) and rarely include explicit auditable constraints, limiting realism. Here, auditability means a monitor can verify compliance from sampler-visible telemetry - e.g., logged replay index/label statistics - by checking that the realized replay class histogram stays close to a nominal baseline and that replay rate is unchanged per batch and/or over a rolling window. We study a limited-privilege insider who controls only replay index selection, not pixels, labels, or model parameters, while staying within auditable limits such as queue priorities. We introduce Amnesia, a replay composition attack that maximizes degradation under two budgets: a visibility budget delta bounding the TV/KL divergence from a nominal class histogram p0, and a mass budget f fixing the replay rate. Amnesia has two steps: (i) compute lightweight class utilities, such as EMA loss or confidence, to tilt p0 toward harmful classes; and (ii) project the tilt back into the delta-ball using efficient KL (exponential tilt) or TV (balanced mass redistribution) optimizers. A windowed scheduler enforces rolling audits. Across challenging CL benchmarks and strong replay baselines, Amnesia consistently lowers final accuracy (ACC) and worsens backward transfer (-BWT). The KL variant delivers high impact while remaining largely undetected under multiple audit schemes, including per-batch and rolling-window checks. The TV variant is more damaging but easier to detect, especially under tight per-class constraints. These results expose index-only replay control as a practical, auditable threat surface in CL systems and establish a principled impact-visibility trade-off.

</details>


### [196] [CAPED: Context-Aware Privacy Exposure Defense for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12666)
*Siyu Shen,Fenghao Xu,Wenrui Diao,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPED，一种面向移动GUI代理的上下文感知预上传曝光控制层，在截图上传至远程多模态代理前，选择性暴露任务所需内容并屏蔽无关隐私信息，在降低隐私泄露的同时保持较高任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于截图的移动GUI代理在执行任务时，每张屏幕截图都可能暴露联系人、消息、照片等与用户请求无关的敏感信息，即"偶然性视觉隐私暴露"问题。现有文本匿名化方法无法应对视觉和推断性线索，而通用隐私遮蔽会移除GUI代理完成任务所需的证据和控件。

Method: CAPED作为手机端保护层，在截图发布到远程多模态代理之前，提取任务需求、利用屏幕上下文作为隐私先验、解析可见UI元素，选择性地仅暴露当前任务所需的内容，同时屏蔽偶然的隐私内容。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上进行28个任务的种子隐私评估中，完整版CAPED将成功条件下的加权种子泄露从原始截图的0.766降至0.268，同时保持了较高的任务效用。更广泛的AndroidWorld运行显示出原型级别的效用成本，但结果支持核心主张。

Conclusion: 截图上传应被视为明确的设备-云端边界决策，应由任务驱动的选择性曝光而非全有或全无的屏幕共享来管理。

Abstract: Screenshot-based mobile GUI agents can operate ordinary smartphone apps through the same visual interface as a human user, but this capability also turns every screen observation into a privacy boundary. During normal task execution, screenshots may expose contacts, messages, photos, files, recommendations, health cues, and other sensitive context that is unrelated to the user's request. We call this problem incidental visual privacy exposure. It is difficult to address with existing defenses: text anonymization misses many visual and inferential cues, while generic privacy masking can remove the evidence and controls that a GUI agent needs to complete the task.
  This paper presents CAPED, a context-aware pre-upload exposure control layer for mobile GUI agents. CAPED is designed as a phone-side protection layer: before screenshots are released to a remote multimodal agent, it extracts task requirements, uses screen context as a privacy prior, parses visible UI elements, and selectively exposes only content needed for the current task while masking incidental private content. We evaluate CAPED on AndroidWorld for broad task utility and with a controlled 28-task seeded privacy evaluation used as a measurement instrument for trajectory-level incidental leakage. In this seeded evaluation, Full CAPED reduces success-conditioned weighted seeded leakage from 0.766 under raw screenshots to 0.268 while preserving high task utility. A broader AndroidWorld run shows a remaining prototype-level utility cost, but the results support the central claim that screenshot upload should be treated as an explicit device--cloud boundary decision, governed by task-driven selective exposure rather than all-or-nothing screen sharing.

</details>


### [197] [SMSR: Certified Defence Against Runtime Memory Poisoning in Persistent LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12703)
*Tarun Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多会话记忆投毒（MSMP）攻击的防御方法——带签名记忆与平滑检索（SMSR），通过写时HMAC-SHA256溯源认证和查询时随机记忆消融+基于裁决的多数投票，首次为该设定提供可证明的鲁棒性保证。实验表明，该防御在15个企业场景中将未签名攻击成功率从93-100%降至0%，对单次注入的认证攻击者将成功率控制在8.0%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）代理通常跨用户会话维护持久记忆，这引入了新的攻击面：攻击者可通过正常交互注入精心构造的记忆，在后续检索时操控代理对无辜用户的响应，而无需修改模型权重或代码。现有防御（如RobustRAG、ReliabilityRAG）假设知识库是静态的，启发式过滤器则容易被流畅的企业风格文本绕过，因此亟需一种可认证鲁棒性的防御方法。

Method: SMSR包含两个组件：组件1在写记忆时添加HMAC-SHA256溯源签名，阻断未签名注入；组件2在查询时应用随机记忆消融（随机移除部分记忆）并结合基于裁决的多数投票，以约束认证攻击者的影响。理论贡献包括：证明无溯源机制的检索时过滤器无法抵御自适应注入；推导出组件2的超几何认证证书；形式化了一致性少数效应（Consistent Minority Effect），即一致对抗性答案在基于字符串投票中可能以少数胜出，而基于裁决的投票可消除此问题。

Result: 组件1在15个企业场景（3150次重复试验）中将所有未签名变种的攻击成功率从93-100%降至0%。对于单次注入的认证攻击者，组件2将成功率控制在8.0%（95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450），低于认证最坏情况。在端到端纯查询攻击中（代理自身而非预植入写入毒记忆），SMSR将成功率从65.3%降至5.3%（n=150，置信区间无重叠）。清洁查询效用为组件1 90%、联合组件85%。

Conclusion: SMSR是首个针对多会话记忆投毒攻击提供可证明鲁棒性边界的防御方法，通过写时签名和查询时随机消融+裁决投票的组合，有效防御未签名和认证的注入攻击，同时保持较高的清洁查询效用。该工作填补了RAG代理持久记忆场景下安全防御的理论空白。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) agents increasingly run with persistent memory that accumulates across user sessions. This creates a new attack surface: an adversary interacting only through normal channels can inject crafted memories that, once retrieved, steer the agent's responses for future users, without touching model weights or code. We call this Multi-Session Memory Poisoning (MSMP) and show that no existing defence certifies against it; static-corpus defences (RobustRAG, ReliabilityRAG) assume a fixed knowledge base, and heuristic filters are bypassed by fluent enterprise-style text. We present Signed Memory with Smoothed Retrieval (SMSR), the first defence with a certified robustness bound for this setting. Component 1 adds HMAC-SHA256 provenance at write time, blocking unsigned injection. Component 2 applies randomised memory ablation with verdict-based majority voting at query time, bounding the influence of authenticated adversaries. We prove that no provenance-free retrieval-time filter can certify against adaptive injection, derive a hypergeometric certificate for Component 2, and formalise the Consistent Minority Effect, whereby a consistent adversarial answer wins string-based voting as a numerical minority while verdict-based voting removes it. Across 15 enterprise scenarios (3,150 repeated trials), Component 1 cuts attack success from 93-100% to 0% for all unsigned variants. For an authenticated adversary with a single injection, Component 2 holds success to 8.0% (95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450), below the certified worst case. In an end-to-end query-only attack where the agent itself writes the poison rather than it being pre-seeded, SMSR reduces success from 65.3% to 5.3% (n=150, non-overlapping CIs) on a live agent stack. Clean-query utility is 90% (Component 1) and 85% (combined).

</details>


### [198] [DIG: Oracle-Guided Directed Input Generation for One-Day Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13037)
*Andrew Bao,Haochen Zeng,Peng Chen,Stephen McCamant,Pen-Chung Yew*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DIG提出了一种利用补丁信息生成漏洞PoC的方法，通过LLM分析补丁合成oracle，结合高层oracle引导的生成器进化和低层分支距离引导的定向模糊测试，在138个真实CVE上超越12个基线工具，触发80个漏洞，其中9个仅DIG能触发，并发现6个未知零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单日漏洞因补丁延迟或不完整部署而构成重大风险，生成PoC输入对评估实际影响至关重要，但现有定向模糊测试方法未能有效识别和求解必要约束，智能体方法在长程推理中存在目标漂移问题。

Method: DIG利用单日漏洞的关键特性（补丁常揭示触发漏洞的必要前置条件），使用LLM分析补丁并合成oracle以显式化这些条件。高层通过oracle引导的生成器进化，让智能体推理并求解约束以满足oracle；低层将oracle植入目标程序，利用分支距离反馈引导定向模糊测试的随机变异。

Result: 在138个真实CVE上，DIG触发80个漏洞，超越最佳基线40%（57 vs. 80），其中9个漏洞仅DIG能触发；92.9%的案例中比基线更快，48.8%的案例提速超100倍，最大提速3,664倍；此外发现6个之前未知的零日漏洞。

Conclusion: DIG通过显式化并有效求解漏洞触发约束，显著提升了单日漏洞PoC生成效率，并具备零日漏洞发现能力。

Abstract: One-day vulnerabilities pose significant risks due to delayed or incomplete patch adoption. Generating proof-of-concept (PoC) inputs is therefore essential for assessing real-world impact. The key challenge is identifying necessary constraints for triggering the vulnerability and solving them effectively. Existing directed fuzzing approaches prioritize inputs toward target locations, but neither explicitly identify necessary constraints nor solve them effectively, relying instead on target-distance feedback and random mutation. Agentic approaches show strong potential through code reasoning and structured input generation, but goal drift in long-horizon reasoning limits their effectiveness.
  DIG addresses this challenge by exploiting a key property of one-day vulnerabilities: patches often reveal necessary preconditions for triggering. DIG uses an LLM to analyze the patch and synthesize an oracle making these conditions explicit. The oracle supports effective PoC generation at two levels. At the high level, DIG performs oracle-guided generator evolution, where an agent infers and solves constraints to satisfy the oracle. At the low level, DIG instruments the oracle into the target program and uses branch-distance feedback to guide random mutation in directed fuzzing. Evaluation shows DIG outperforms 2 state-of-the-art agents and 10 fuzzers across 138 real-world CVEs. DIG triggers 80 vulnerabilities, surpassing prior results and outperforming the best baseline by 40% (57 vs. 80 CVEs). Notably, DIG exclusively triggers 9 vulnerabilities no existing technique can trigger. Compared to the average of other tools, DIG triggers vulnerabilities faster in 92.9% of cases, achieving over 100x speedup in 48.8% of cases, with a maximum speedup of 3,664x. Beyond one-day PoC generation, DIG uncovers 6 previously unknown vulnerabilities in widely deployed libraries, enabling zero-day discovery.

</details>


### [199] [PI-Hunter: Automated Red-Teaming for Exposing and Localizing Prompt Injections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12737)
*Pengfei He,Lesly Miculicich,Vishesh Sharma,Ash Fox,George Lee,Jiliang Tang,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PI-Hunter，一种自动化审计框架，通过构建源感知测试用例并迭代演化，主动暴露LLM智能体中的潜在提示注入漏洞，显著提升漏洞暴露和攻击面覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要聚焦推理时阻断恶意内容，红队方法仅优化攻击成功率，导致开发者对潜在提示注入如何在智能体中产生和传播的可见性不足。

Method: 构建具有源感知的测试用例，通过反馈驱动的探索迭代演化，诱导智能体检索并揭示嵌入在外部环境中的恶意指令。

Result: 在多个基准、智能体架构、攻击和防御场景下，PI-Hunter相比强自动化红队基线显著提升了漏洞暴露和攻击面覆盖率，且在现有提示注入防御下仍保持有效。

Conclusion: PI-Hunter为LLM智能体提供了一种主动漏洞暴露的有效审计方法，弥补了现有防御和红队方法的不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into agentic systems that interact with external tools and environments, introducing new security risks such as indirect prompt injection attacks through untrusted external sources. Existing defenses mainly focus on blocking malicious content at inference time, and current red-teaming methods primarily optimize attack success. As a result, developers have limited visibility into how latent prompt injections emerge and propagate through agents. We propose PI-Hunter, an automated agentic auditing framework for proactive vulnerability exposure in LLM agents. PI-Hunter constructs realistic source-aware test cases and iteratively evolves them through feedback-driven exploration to induce agents to retrieve and reveal latent malicious instructions embedded within external environments. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks, agent architectures, attacks, and defenses demonstrate that PI-Hunter substantially improves vulnerability exposure and attack-surface coverage over strong automated red-teaming baselines, while remaining effective under existing prompt injection defenses.

</details>


### [200] [Semantic Identification of IoT Devices from Behavioral Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12793)
*Samuel Witt,Hassan Habibi Gharakheili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于制造商使用描述（MUD）配置文件的物联网设备识别方法，通过访问控制条目（ACE）级别的语义表示进行设备识别。实验表明，在运行时观测存在稀疏重叠或早期阶段时，语义ACE匹配比精确ACE匹配更有效，能够保留更多识别证据并保持较高排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物联网设备识别方法依赖数据包或流记录的低级通信观测，其流量模式会因部署环境、软件版本和用户交互而变化，导致识别不稳定。本文利用MUD配置文件中的ACE语义信息进行设备识别，以克服上述局限。

Method: 首先从28个公开MUD配置文件中提取1,023个ACE实例，构建ACE级语义表示并分析其几何特性；然后评估语义ACE匹配在多种运行时变化（未见ACE、主机名漂移、部分观测）下的表现；最后在超过80万条真实物联网流量轨迹上进行验证。

Result: ACE级语义表示比整体配置文件嵌入更能保持设备级行为区分度，经白化校准后仍然有效。在运行时观测中，精确ACE匹配在高重叠时表现良好，但重叠稀疏或消失时性能急剧下降；语义ACE匹配在各种条件下均能保留有用识别证据。在真实流量中，精确重叠在有稳定重叠时最强，而语义ACE匹配在观测早期和稀疏重叠时提供更强的识别证据。

Conclusion: 基于ACE语义表示的物联网设备识别方法在运行时观测不完整或早期阶段比传统精确匹配更具鲁棒性，为实际部署中的设备识别提供了有效补充。

Abstract: Accurate identification of IoT devices is important for security management and policy enforcement. Existing approaches typically learn device signatures from packets or flow records. These methods operate on low-level communication observations whose traffic patterns may vary across deployments, software versions, and user interactions. This paper studies device identification using Manufacturer Usage Description (MUD) profiles. MUD profiles describe device behavior using Access Control Entries (ACEs), where each ACE represents a behavioral primitive consisting of protocol, endpoint, direction, and port semantics derived from device communication policy. Our contributions are threefold. First, using 28 publicly available MUD profiles containing 1,023 ACE instances, we construct ACE-level semantic representations from compact behavioral text and analyze their geometric properties. ACE-level representations preserve device-level behavioral distinctions more effectively than whole-profile embeddings and remain effective after whitening calibration. Second, we evaluate semantic ACE matching under controlled runtime variations, including unseen ACEs, drifted hostnames, and partial runtime observation. Exact ACE matching performs well when the overlap with the canonical MUD profile remains high, but degrades sharply when the overlap becomes sparse or disappears. In contrast, semantic ACE matching preserves useful identification evidence across these conditions. Third, we evaluate the same approach on real IoT traffic traces comprising more than 800,000 observed flows. Exact overlap remains the strongest signal when stable overlap exists, while semantic ACE matching provides stronger identification evidence during the early stages of observation, frequently retains the correct device among the highest-ranked candidates, and remains effective under sparse-overlap runtime traffic.

</details>


### [201] [Who Pays the Price? Stakeholder-Centric Prompt Injection Benchmarking for Real-world Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13385)
*Zihao Wang,Yiming Li,Yutong Wu,Zheyu Liu,Kangjie Chen,Fok Kar Wai,Pin-Yu Chen,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing,Bo Li,Dacheng Tao,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了StakeBench，一个以利益相关者为中心的基准测试，用于系统性地分类和归因LLM驱动的Web代理在真实环境中因提示注入攻击产生的危害，并揭示了当前代理无法可靠抵抗任何攻击目标、故障模式多样且被传统评估忽略的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准采用攻击中心视角，只关注注入的技术可行性，忽略了危害在不同利益相关者（如用户、卖家、平台）间的不对称分布以及同一攻击模式对不同目标的差异化效果。

Method: 构建利益相关者中心基准StakeBench，区分受影响实体（用户、卖家、平台），将攻击分解为具体目标，并使用结果级和过程级互补指标进行评估。

Result: 当前代理无法可靠抵抗任何攻击目标；故障模式可分为隐身寄生（攻击成功且不干扰用户任务）、错位干扰（任务被干扰但攻击未成功）和复合失败（攻击目标和任务完整性同时被违反）等定性不同模式。

Conclusion: 传统评估遗漏了这些模式，突显了在真实部署中对LLM代理进行利益相关者感知评估的必要性。

Abstract: Web agents driven by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world environments, where they operate over untrusted web content and execute actions with direct consequences. This makes them vulnerable to prompt-injection attacks, in which seemingly benign content embeds adversarial instructions that manipulate agent behaviour. Existing security benchmarks adopt an \textit{attack-centric} perspective, focusing on the technical feasibility of injections while overlooking the nuanced distribution of resulting harms. In practice, however, prompt-injection risk is victim-dependent: a single exploit can produce asymmetric consequences for different stakeholders, and the same attack pattern may exhibit substantially different effectiveness depending on whom it targets. To capture these properties, we introduce \textbf{\sysname}, a \textit{stakeholder-centric} benchmark to systematically categorize and attribute harm in real-world web agent systems. It distinguishes between affected entities (e.g., user, seller, platform), decomposes the attacks into concrete objectives, and evaluates each case with complementary outcome- and process-level metrics. Our results reveal substantial and heterogeneous vulnerabilities: not a single attack objective is reliably resisted by current agents, and failures distribute across qualitatively distinct modes ranging from \emph{stealthy parasitism} (attack succeeds without disrupting the user's delegated task) to \emph{misaligned disruption} (task disrupted without attack success) and \emph{compounded failure} (both adversarial objective and task integrity simultaneously violated). These patterns are missed by conventional evaluation, highlighting the need for stakeholder-aware assessment of LLM-based agents in real-world deployments. Benchmark is available at https://github.com/StakeBench/SBC.

</details>


### [202] [A Privacy-Preserving Framework Using Remote Data Science for Inter-Institutional Student Retention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12845)
*John Fields,K M Sajjadul Islam,Ruchitha Thota,Victor Chen,Praveen Madiraju*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究利用PySyft平台开发了远程数据科学（RDS）框架，采用半气隙架构实现多机构间协作预测学生保留率，在不直接访问敏感数据的情况下取得了一致的分类性能（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695），并提出了新型隐私优先的DataType-Aware Templates合成数据方法，验证了RDS-based PPML在教育场景的技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决教育机构间协作预测学生保留率时面临的隐私保护挑战，探索在不共享原始敏感数据的情况下实现跨机构机器学习的技术路径，特别是为小规模机构间合作提供联邦学习的实用替代方案。

Method: 基于PySyft平台构建远程数据科学（RDS）框架，采用半气隙架构（高低侧服务器）；评估三种合成数据生成方法；提出DataType-Aware Templates合成数据方法；在三所大学间进行跨机构协作验证。

Result: 跨机构分类性能一致（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695）；严格符合FERPA合规要求；验证了RDS-based PPML的技术可行性。

Conclusion: RDS-based PPML在教育场景中技术可行，可作为联邦学习在小规模跨机构协作中的实用替代方案；DataType-Aware Templates方法在隐私保护上优于分布保真度。

Abstract: This study explores privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML) techniques using the PySyft platform to enable collaborative prediction of student retention between institutions. We developed a remote data science (RDS) framework with a semi-air-gapped architecture consisting of high-side and low-side servers, allowing researchers from three universities to build predictive models on sensitive student data without direct data access. Using historical data from a small private university (N=720), we evaluated three synthetic data generation approaches and validated the framework through inter-institutional collaboration. The results demonstrate consistent classification performance across institutions (Macro F1: 0.690--0.695) while maintaining strict Family Educational Rights and Privacy Act (FERPA) compliance. We also propose Data-Type-Aware Templates, a novel synthetic data method that prioritizes privacy over distributional fidelity. Our findings confirm that RDS-based PPML is technically feasible for educational settings and offers a practical alternative to federated learning for small-scale inter-institutional collaborations. The code is available at https://github.com/jtfields/NAIRR240195-Privacy-Preserving-Machine-Learning.

</details>


### [203] [LNTest: A Testbed for Evaluating Bitcoin Lightning Network-Based Botnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12887)
*Thomas Bakaysa,Ahmet Kurt,Abdul-Salem Beibitkhan,Jesus Maria Romo Diaz de Leon,Tag Kalat,Joshua Kramer,Estela Rodriguez,Abraham Watkins,Abdullah Aydeger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了LNTest——首个用于LN僵尸网络的可重用测试平台，揭示了D-LNBot自主形成协议产生的是聚类链（而非均匀链），命令传播复杂度为Θ(n)（而非O(m log n)），且不同覆盖拓扑对拆除策略的抵抗力差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LNBot和D-LNBot仅为概念验证原型，仅通过仿真评估，未解决真实世界拓扑形成、传播复杂性和抗拆除能力等关键问题。

Method: 基于Core Lightning节点构建容器化测试平台LNTest，支持三种覆盖拓扑模式（确定性链、自主发现、用户提供图），在共享Bitcoin Core regtest链上运行实验。

Result: D-LNBot自主形成产生聚类链（桥节点连接团簇）；命令传播线性增长Θ(n)；均匀链抗定向拆除但易随机失败，无标度拓扑模式相反，自主聚类链在两种攻击下均脆弱。

Conclusion: LNTest作为开源可复现测试平台，支持LN僵尸网络防御的可重复研究。

Abstract: Bitcoin's Lightning Network (LN) can be exploited as a covert, low-cost command-and-control (C&C) channel for botnets, as demonstrated by the LNBot and D-LNBot designs. However, both remain proof-of-concept prototypes evaluated only through simulation, leaving key questions about real-world topology formation, propagation complexity, and resilience to takedowns unanswered. We present LNTest, the first reusable testbed for LN-based botnets, built from Core Lightning nodes containerized with Docker over a shared Bitcoin Core regtest chain. LNTest supports three overlay topology modes (a deterministic chain, autonomous peer discovery, and user-supplied graphs), enabling controlled experiments across different botnet structures. Using LNTest, we report three main findings. First, D-LNBot's autonomous formation protocol does not produce the uniform chain from its design; instead, it creates a clustered chain in which cliques are linked by bridge nodes whose removal fragments the network. Second, command propagation scales linearly with botnet size ($Θ(n)$), not the $O(m \log n)$ previously claimed, and gains nothing from higher neighbor connectivity. Third, the overlay topology determines the effectiveness of takedown strategies: uniform-degree chains resist targeted removal but fragment under random failure, scale-free topologies show the opposite pattern, and the autonomous clustered chain is fragile under both, making it the most vulnerable of the three. LNTest is released as open source, with a script that reproduces all our experiments, to support reproducible research on LN-based botnet defenses.

</details>


### [204] [MAStrike: Shapley-Guided Collusive Red-Teaming on Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12918)
*Chejian Xu,Zhaorun Chen,Jingyang Zhang,Freddy Lecue,Avni Kothari,Sarah Tan,Wenbo Guo,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MAStrike，一个用于层级多智能体系统的闭环联合红队测试框架，通过首次提出的智能体级Shapley值分析量化各智能体对系统鲁棒性的边际贡献，识别脆弱智能体联盟并生成协调的角色感知对抗攻击，在金融、软件工程和CRM等多个领域的实验中显著优于启发式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层级多智能体系统的红队测试方法存在局限：依赖启发式选择目标智能体并扰动孤立消息流，无法回答哪些智能体对系统安全负责最大，以及被攻陷的智能体如何协调绕过防御。随着MAS在高风险场景中的快速部署，这种局限性导致安全漏洞被忽视。

Method: 首次提出面向MAS的智能体级Shapley值分析，量化各智能体在任务特定分布下对系统鲁棒性的边际贡献；基于此归因识别脆弱智能体联盟，生成协调的、角色感知的对抗操作；通过结构化因果诊断迭代优化攻击，将失败案例归因于阻碍攻击的未攻陷智能体；构建涵盖多种层级拓扑和领域的综合MAS红队测试基准。

Result: 在多个前沿模型构建的MAS上，MAStrike显著优于启发式基线；揭示了非平凡的Shapley值分布和智能体间高阶交互结构，发现了被先前单智能体或模板方法忽视的关键漏洞和协调模式。

Conclusion: MAStrike为层级MAS的安全评估提供了一种系统性的归因驱动红队测试框架，能够有效发现多智能体系统中的协同攻击路径和关键脆弱节点，对保障MAS在高风险场景中的安全部署具有重要价值。

Abstract: Hierarchical multi-agent systems (MAS) are rapidly being deployed in high-stakes workflows across domains such as finance and software engineering. In these systems, safety and security are inherently distributed across role-specialized agents, significantly expanding the attack surface, particularly under coordinated adversarial behaviors such as privilege escalation and cross-agent collusion. Existing red-teaming approaches for MAS remain limited: they rely on heuristic selection of target agents and perturb isolated message streams, leaving critical questions unanswered as which agents are most responsible for system safety, and how compromised agents can coordinate to bypass defenses. We propose MAStrike, a closed-loop framework for collusive red-teaming in hierarchical MAS. We propose the first agent-level Shapley value analysis for MAS, quantifying each agent's marginal contribution to system robustness under task-specific distributions. GGuided by this attribution, MAStrike identifies vulnerable agent coalitions and generates coordinated, role-aware adversarial manipulations. These attacks are iteratively refined through structured causal diagnosis, attributing failure cases to uncompromised agents that block adversarial attempts. We further build a comprehensive MAS red-teaming benchmark and controllable environments spanning diverse hierarchical topologies and domains, including finance, software engineering, and CRM. Extensive experiments across MAS built on multiple frontier models show that MAStrike substantially outperforms heuristic baselines. Our analysis further uncovers non-trivial Shapley value distributions and higher-order interaction structures among agents, revealing critical vulnerabilities and coordination patterns that are overlooked by prior single-agent or template-based methods.

</details>


### [205] [ViPER: Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12949)
*Fatima Qaiser,Bisma Tahir,Muhammad Abid Mughal,Nauman Shamim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ViPER是一种基于视觉的打包感知编码器，用于鲁棒恶意软件检测。它采用LoRA适配的ViT-B/14骨干网络和双头架构，联合学习恶意软件分类和打包检测，通过打包感知门控机制和频率加权损失处理打包样本，在20万Windows PE字节图图像上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可视化的恶意软件检测方法在面对可执行文件打包时存在关键失败模式：打包后的二进制文件产生高熵图像，掩盖了模型依赖的结构模式。由于打包在良性软件中也普遍存在，打包状态本身不能作为恶意指示器，现有方法未能在统一监督框架下解决这一挑战。

Method: ViPER基于LoRA适配的ViT-B/14骨干网络，采用双头架构联合学习恶意软件分类和打包检测。通过打包感知门控机制，根据推断的打包状态条件化恶意软件预测，为打包和未打包输入建立不同的决策边界。训练中使用频率加权损失和分层采样来处理打包标签偏斜问题。

Result: 在20万Windows PE字节图图像上，ViPER的平衡准确率为0.8521，ROC-AUC为0.9260，AUPR为0.9279，在所有主要指标上优于代表性SOTA基线，打包检测AUC达到0.9949。

Conclusion: ViPER通过打包感知的视觉架构有效解决了打包二进制文件对基于图像的恶意软件检测带来的挑战，验证了在统一监督框架下联合建模打包状态和恶意属性的有效性。

Abstract: Visualization-based malware detection maps raw binary bytes to grayscale images and applies learned visual classifiers, providing an evasion-resistant and disassembly-free alternative to conventional analysis pipelines. However, executable packing remains a critical failure mode: packed binaries produce high-entropy images that obscure the structural patterns these models rely on. Because packing is also prevalent in benign software (e.g., for compression or copy protection), packing state alone is not a reliable indicator of maliciousness, and existing approaches do not address this challenge within a unified supervised framework. We present ViPER, a Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust malware detection. ViPER builds on a LoRA-adapted ViT-B/14 backbone with a dual-head architecture that jointly learns malware classification and packing detection. A packing-aware gating mechanism conditions malware predictions on the inferred packing state, enabling distinct decision boundaries for packed and unpacked inputs. To address packing label skew during training, we employ frequency-weighted losses with stratified sampling over joint class-packing strata. Evaluated on 200,000 Windows PE byteplot images, ViPER achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.8521, ROC-AUC of 0.9260, and AUPR of 0.9279, outperforming representative state-of-the-art baselines across all primary metrics, while attaining a packing detection AUC of 0.9949.

</details>


### [206] [SoK: The Constant Time Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13000)
*Billy Bob Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化整理了常量时间编程模型及其演进，发现模型保护范围与规范假设之间存在持续差距，并提出一种攻击方法论用于发现密码学原语边界之外的时序漏洞。利用该方法，发现了私钥加载相关的规范级漏洞，并在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中确认了该泄漏。反直觉的是，尽管BoringSSL威胁模型更严格，其单次观测信号强度反而比OpenSSL高出数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"常量时间"定义在学术界和工业界存在分歧，且时序漏洞可能源自密码学原语边界之外（如规范层面），而现有防御手段未覆盖这些区域，因此需要系统化梳理模型并开发新的攻击方法论。

Method: 本文系统化整理了常量时间模型及其演变过程，识别出模型保护范围与规范假设之间的差距，并提炼出一种攻击方法论，用于发现密码学原始边界之外的时序漏洞。

Result: 发现了一个与私钥加载相关的规范级时序漏洞，并在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中确认了泄漏。意外发现BoringSSL的单次观测信号强度比OpenSSL高出数个数量级，尽管其威胁模型更为严格。

Conclusion: 常量时间模型与实际规范之间存在系统性差距，即使威胁模型更严格的实现（如BoringSSL）也可能存在更强的侧信道泄漏，这凸显了在密码学原语边界之外进行时序安全性分析的重要性。

Abstract: Constant time programming patterns is the primary defense against timing attacks on cryptographic implementations, yet what "constant time" means varies across academia and industry. This work systematizes constant time models and their evolution, identifies a recurring gap between what models protect and what specifications assume, and distills an offensive methodology for discovering timing vulnerabilities that originate outside the cryptographic primitive boundary. Applying this methodology, we locate a specification-level vulnerability related to private key loading, and confirm the leak in both OpenSSL and BoringSSL. Counterintuitively, BoringSSL's per-observation signal is several orders of magnitude stronger than OpenSSL's, despite an explicitly stricter threat model.

</details>


### [207] [The Emergence of Autonomous Penetration Capabilities in Large Language Model-Powered AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13079)
*Jiaqi Luo,Jiarun Dai,Zhile Chen,Jia Xu,Weibing Wang,Yawen Duan,Brian Tse,Geng Hong,Xudong Pan,Yuan Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含两层目标环境（Tier-1和Tier-2，共300台目标服务器）和通用智能体架构的自主渗透评估框架，在无目标先验知识条件下评估了19个开源和闭源LLM，发现当前模型的渗透成功率为10.7%~69.3%，且自主渗透能力随模型整体能力提升而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主渗透能力评估存在方法论不透明、测试场景过于简化或 unrealistic、以及向LLM提供过多先验知识和任务特定指导等问题，无法准确衡量AI系统在现实高影响网络攻击场景中自主执行渗透这一核心能力。

Method: 构建包含两部分的新评估框架：(1) 目标服务器端：基于安全服务数量设计Tier-1（1个安全服务）和Tier-2（3个安全服务）两层环境，共300台目标服务器；(2) 智能体脚手架：采用通用智能体架构，配备通用网络安全工具集，无目标特定先验知识。评估19个开源和闭源LLM。

Result: 当前模型自主渗透成功率为10.7%至69.3%，且自主渗透能力随模型整体能力提升而持续改善。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能更准确评估LLM在真实网络攻击场景中的自主渗透能力，揭示当前模型在此任务上能力有限但持续提升的趋势。

Abstract: Nowadays, the autonomous execution of cyberattacks capable of causing substantial real-world harm is widely regarded as one of the critical red lines that frontier AI systems must not cross. Within this broader red-line scenario, autonomous penetration represents a core enabling capability and subtask: the ability of LLM-powered AI systems to independently conduct adversarial operations against a target server without human intervention, identify and exploit vulnerabilities, and obtain unauthorized access or control. A growing body of work has sought to assess the autonomous penetration capabilities of AI systems. However, existing evaluations often employ opaque methodologies, rely on unrealistic or overly simplified penetration-testing scenarios, or provide LLMs with excessive prior knowledge and task-specific guidance, and cannot accurately capture the extent to which modern AI systems can autonomously perform this core capability within broader high-impact cyberattack scenarios.
  To address these limitations, we construct a new autonomous penetration evaluation framework consisting of two components: target servers and agent scaffolding. Specifically, on the target-server side, we design two levels of target environments based on the number of secure services without known vulnerabilities deployed alongside a vulnerable service: Tier~1 (one secure service) and Tier~2 (three secure services), resulting in a total of 300 target servers. Meanwhile, the agent scaffolding adopts a general-purpose agent architecture equipped with a set of general-purpose cybersecurity tools, without any target-specific prior knowledge. We evaluate 19 open-weight and proprietary LLMs, and find that current models achieve penetration success rates ranging from 10.7% to 69.3%. Moreover, we observe that autonomous penetration capability continues to improve alongside advances in overall model capability.

</details>


### [208] [The Invisible Ink of the Android Malware World: A Longitudinal Study on the Usage of Covert Communication Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13107)
*Zeya Umayya,Manan Aggarwal,Manan Chugh,Mann Nariya,Yogesh Kaushik,Sambuddho Chakravarty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对Android恶意软件生态系统中隐蔽通道（CC）的使用进行了纵向研究，分析了2009年至2025年间350万恶意APK样本，发现CC使用率从2012年的0.30%飙升至2025年的50%，识别出511个恶意软件家族中的288K个APK使用了CC，并通过动态分析发现了19,308个唯一IP地址。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管隐蔽通道被恶意软件和僵尸网络滥用以逃避检测，但缺乏针对恶意软件如何利用隐蔽通道的纵向研究，这一关键空白亟待填补。

Method: 开发了一个结合静态与动态分析的多阶段分析管道，对350万Android恶意软件样本（2009-2025年）进行系统和网络级特征分析，并实施了长达16年的纵向数据集研究。

Result: 发现288K APK（511个家族）使用CC，使用率从2012年的0.30%指数级增长至2025年的50%；动态分析识别出85个国家的19,308个唯一IP，其中17个国家的59个IP被验证存在CC；发现CC使用模式演化，如多CC并行使用和周期性切换（某家族在2019-2025年间切换CC达40次）。

Conclusion: Android恶意软件中隐蔽通道的使用呈指数级增长且高度演化，亟需针对性的检测与防御策略。

Abstract: Proxies, VPNs and Tor have long helped the privacy community and users in censored regions to fight censorship. However, the same tools can be maliciously exploited by malware and botnets to conceal their communication to external command and control servers. Despite being a critical concern fueled by the proliferation of malware based attacks, no longitudinal studies have analyzed how malware applications use covert channels (CC) to evade detection.
  We fill this gap by performing the first study of the usage of covert channels in the Android malware ecosystem. To that end, we develop a multistage pipeline that combines static and dynamic analysis to investigate both system and network-level features. We applied this pipeline on a corpus of 3.5M Android malware spanning 2009 to July 2025. Our carefully crafted static validation rules uncovered 288K APKs that used CCs spanning 511 malware families and CC usage growing exponentially from 0.30\% (2012) to 50\% (2025). Overall, in dynamic analysis, we identified 19,308 unique IP addresses being contacted in 85 countries, out of which we were able to explicitly validate the presence of CCs for 59 IP addresses across 17 countries. Further, we performed a longitudinal dataset study spanning over 16 years for CC based malware and found that CC usage has evolved, \textit{e.g.,} some malware adopted by using more than one CCs; others switched between them periodically (one family switched CC usage 40 times from 2019 to 2025).

</details>


### [209] [An Assessment Framework for Application-Level Cryptographic Agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13425)
*Navaneeth Rameshan,Gregoire Messmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个七维组件化评估框架，用于系统评估应用程序级的密码敏捷性。通过对六个代表性API的分析，发现了三个普遍存在的独立缺口：缺乏基于意图的密钥创建、缺乏策略驱动的算法选择（区别于访问控制）、以及缺乏针对现有密钥的算法转换操作。这些缺口单独就足以阻碍敏捷迁移，解释了为何后量子密码迁移在API发展数十年后仍是一个软件工程难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前密码学API在设计时未考虑敏捷性，导致后量子密码迁移面临挑战。缺乏系统性评估密码敏捷性的方法，且"密码敏捷性"这一术语本身包含多种独立能力（算法替换、策略选择、实现替换），缺乏结构化分解限制了系统评估和API开发。

Method: 提出了一个基于组件的评估框架，从七个正交维度表征应用级密码敏捷性：三个耦合维度（应用代码对算法和提供者的了解程度）、一个横切割耦合机制、一个治理权威维度、以及两个敏捷性使能维度（实际迁移能力）。该框架是非线性的，支持非层次化的配置文件。对六个代表性API（PKCS#11、OpenSSL 3.0、JCA、Google Tink、AWS KMS、HashiCorp Vault Transit）进行了评估。

Result: 发现了三个普遍存在且相互独立的缺口：1) 没有系统支持基于意图的密钥创建；2) 没有系统提供策略驱动的算法选择（区别于访问控制）；3) 没有系统提供针对现有密钥的算法转换专用操作。每个缺口单独就足以阻碍敏捷迁移。

Conclusion: 尽管API在数十年间取得了进展，但当前密码学API在敏捷性方面存在根本性缺陷，这解释了为何后量子密码迁移仍是一个软件工程问题。框架揭示了API设计中亟待解决的关键缺口，为构建真正密码敏捷的系统指明了方向。

Abstract: The impending post-quantum transition to new cryptography will require complete replacement of algorithms within all software. The cryptographic APIs used today make this transition challenging because they were not designed with agility as a concern. There is no method for systematically assessing cryptographic agility as an overall ability. In addition to this, the term itself refers to multiple independent capabilities. Specifically, it includes replacing algorithms, selecting by policy, and substituting implementations. This lack of structured decomposition limits both the evaluation of systems and the development of cryptographically agile APIs.
  We introduce a component-based assessment framework that characterizes application-level cryptographic agility along seven orthogonal dimensions: three coupling dimensions that measure what the application code knows about algorithms and providers, a cross-cutting decoupling mechanism, a governance authority dimension, and two agility enablers that measure actual migration capability. The framework is non-linear and captures non-hierarchical profiles: a system may achieve high operation decoupling yet low creation decoupling, or strong versioning without externalized configuration.
